## Baseline entries

### L-0001 - Exact target and kernel

The target is uniform on identity transposition words Omega(N,m), for N>=3 and
even m>=4. The kernel is the explicitly lazy random scan of exact
same-product windows of sizes 3,4,5, restricted to sizes at most m, with the
normalization in GOAL_SPEC.md. Terminal claims must concern this exact chain.

Verdict: PROVED DEFINITION.

### L-0002 - Stationarity

Each window update is a Gibbs conditional for the uniform bridge law.
Therefore the exact random-scan mixture is reversible and stationary. Laziness
removes periodicity. Irreducibility and mixing remain separate.

Evidence:

Pure Hurwitz moves preserve labeled weighted support-component data and cannot
sample globally. Recorded exact cases have 4, 13, and 17 Hurwitz components for
S_3,m=4; S_4,m=4; and S_4,m=6 respectively.

Hurwitz-only global-sampling detour.

### L-0004 - Hurwitz classification source

Ben-Itzhak and Teicher, Literature/0110110v1.pdf, Theorem 3.4, states that two
identity factorizations into the same number of transpositions are
Hurwitz-equivalent exactly when their factorization graphs have the same
labeled connected components and matching component weights. Their displayed
Hurwitz generator uses the inverse orientation of another common convention,
which generates the same equivalence relation.

Verdict: CITED; project convention translation and bibliographic audit queued
as M01.

### L-0005 - Feasible identity components

A connected component on v>=2 vertices supports an identity transposition word
of length k exactly when k is even and k>=2(v-1). Doubled trees establish the
minimal construction; square pairs add even surplus.

### L-0013 - Mandated source and convention audit completed

The coordinator read the durable sources in the prompt's required order at
repository commit `502a6f031c6d5a4bc0064ee5b370a4fa3914f993`. The worktree
already contained the untracked `notes/` preparation artifacts; they are being
preserved and extended in place.

Ben-Itzhak--Teicher, arXiv:math/0110110v1, Theorem 3.4, applies to two
factorizations of the identity in `S_N` with the same number of factors and
states equivalence exactly when their factorization graphs have the same
labeled vertex sets in corresponding connected components and the same total
component weights. It allows identity factors, but its proof explicitly
specializes to the transposition-only case, so the project state space is
covered. The paper's generator is

    (a,b) -> (a b a^{-1}, a),

which is the inverse of the project's named right generator

    (a,b) -> (b, b^{-1} a b).

Hence both generate the same equivalence relation. The paper includes all
`N` graph vertices; the project's omission of zero-weight singleton
components is harmless because `N` and all labels are fixed, so those
singletons are recoverable from the nonzero labeled data.

Chen--Haah--Haferkamp--Liu--Metger--Tan, arXiv:2406.07478v3, Theorems 1.1
and 5.2, concern unconditioned moment-operator gaps for random reversible
circuits and strongly explicit implementations of expanding generators.
They do not analyze an endpoint-conditioned word measure or the chain
`K_{N,m}` and therefore provide no direct gap transfer.

The indexed 20260710 work is an unrun placeholder. The 20260721 edge-shift
experiment forgets word order and is outside the target chain.

Verdict: PROVED CONVENTION/CITATION TRANSLATION for Classification H; the
remaining local-chain irreducibility construction is assigned to M01.

### L-0014 - Cycle 01 exact contract and frontier

Target distribution: uniform on `Omega(N,m)` for `N>=3` and even `m>=4`.
Coordinator convention: permutations are maps on `[N]`; `pq` means `p` after
`q`, so `(pq)(x)=p(q(x))`, and a word evaluates to `t_1...t_m` in that
notation. The exact reviewed kernel is

    K_{N,m} = (1/2) I
      + (1/(2|R_m|)) sum_{r in R_m}
          (1/(m-r+1)) sum_{i=1}^{m-r+1} H_{i,r},

where `R_m={r in {3,4,5}: r<=m}` and `H_{i,r}` is uniform on the exact
same-product length-`r` conditional fiber. No modified chain, conditional
fiber, or fixed-parameter result can be terminal.

Cycle 01 begins with two independent counterexample lanes and one resource
lane because only three worker slots are available. The coordinator owns the
independent M01 classification/irreducibility lane. All fifteen initial
missions remain required and will be run in sequential waves.

Verdict: ACTIVE CYCLE START.

### L-0018 - Coordinator M01 qualitative irreducibility proof

The coordinator produced a complete proof at
`experiments/20260811-polynomial-mixing-resolution/cycle-01/mission-M01/`.

   factor, including the right boundary;
2. audited Classification H connects each word to an explicitly constructed
   representative consisting entirely of adjacent square pairs with the same
   labeled component sets and weights;
3. every `(t,t)->(s,s)` refresh is a positive-probability `H_4` update inside
   a containing length-four window, including boundary pairs and `m=4`;
4. refreshing all pairs to a fixed transposition connects every state to one
   universal word, and all moves reverse with positive probability.

The exact lower probabilities for a chosen realized move are
`[2|R_m|(m-2)F_3(h)]^{-1}` and
`[2|R_m|(m-3)F_4(h)]^{-1}` respectively.  These certify positivity only.
The Classification-H path length is not bounded here, and routing through the
universal word would have unacceptable hub congestion.

nonterminal qualitative theorem.

### L-0019 - Coordinator quantitative bridge-path lane

With M01 written, the coordinator begins an independent reformulation of

transposition Cayley graph, with endpoints fixed at the identity.  The target

Verdict: ACTIVE ASSIGNMENT.

### L-0020 - Adjacent-pair bridge-Glauber reduction

Define the auxiliary lazy chain

    J=(1/2)I+(1/(2(m-1))) sum_{j=1}^{m-1} H_{j,2}.

Every off-diagonal `H_{j,2}` transition can be padded on an available side by
one fixed factor and realized by one `H_3` update of `K`.  If `M=binom(N,2)`,
then `F_3(g)=sum_{c in T_N}F_2(gc)<=M^2`, while the old pair ensures
`F_2(p)>=1`.  Comparing the exact scan and heat-bath probabilities gives

    K(x,y) >= [1/(3M^2)] J(x,y)

for every off-diagonal `J` edge, hence

    E_K(f,f) >= [1/(3M^2)] E_J(f,f),
    gap(K) >= [1/(3M^2)] gap(J).

Under prefix coordinates `x_k=t_1...t_k`, `J` is the lazy random-scan
single-site heat bath on length-`m` paths in `Cay(S_N,T_N)` with both endpoints
fixed at identity.  The local two-step fibers have sizes `M` for identity,
`3` for a 3-cycle, and `2` for two disjoint transpositions.

Artifact:
`experiments/20260811-polynomial-mixing-resolution/cycle-01/coordinator-bridge-path/pair_heatbath_reduction.md`.

Verdict: PROVED BY COORDINATOR, PENDING INDEPENDENT AUDIT.  This is a one-way
positive reduction; slow mixing of `J` would not refute fast mixing of `K`.

### L-0021 - Exact stationary adjacent-square frequency

For `M=binom(N,2)` and any fixed adjacent position, insertion/deletion gives

    Pr_pi(t_j=t_{j+1})
      = M |Omega(N,m-2)|/|Omega(N,m)|
      = p_{m-2}/(M p_m),

where `p_k=P^k(id,id)` for simple random transpositions.  Since `P` is a
self-adjoint contraction and `m` is even, its spectral measure gives
`p_m<=p_{m-2}`.  Thus every fixed position is a square with stationary
probability at least `1/M`, and its square label is exactly uniform.  A
stationary `J` step performs a changed square refresh with probability at
least `(1/2)(1/M)(1-1/M)`.

Artifact:
`experiments/20260811-polynomial-mixing-resolution/cycle-01/coordinator-bridge-path/stationary_square_frequency.md`.

Verdict: PROVED AVERAGE-FLOW LEMMA.  It does not give a per-state square,
conductance lower bound for a named cut, or global Poincare inequality.

rejected-exit restrictions.  If `bar_lambda` is the projection gap and
`lambda_min` the minimum restriction gap, then the explicit half-laziness of
`K` gives

    gap(K) >= bar_lambda lambda_min / 3.

This supersedes the separate mean-lift obligation in L-0007.  It does not
permit renormalizing macro-preserving moves: exits must become holding mass,
and a reducible restriction still has gap zero.

The same report proved `gap(K_{N,4})>=1/4` from the whole-word `H_{1,4}`
orthogonal projection.  For `m=2n>=6`, it defined the nonlazy average `B` of
the aligned windows `H_{2j-1,4}`, identified it exactly with the adjacent
two-increment heat bath for the `Q=P_transposition^2` return bridge (including
microfactorization weights), and proved

    gap(K_{N,2n}) >= gap(B_{N,n})/12.

The exact coefficient before simplification is `(m-2)/(12(m-3))`.  Exact
enumeration found `B` connected in the three calibration cases, which is a

### L-0026 - M13 full-support and entropic red-team return

    E_{K^F}(f,f)

The proof allocates oriented generators to padded `H_3` windows, includes the

`F_3<=M^2`, and an allocation multiplicity of at most four.  Classification H
makes `P_H` qualitatively irreducible on each macro-fiber, but gives no gap.

infinite cut candidates with exact conditional masses and boundary flow.  The

`(N-1)!N^(N-1)/(2N-2)!` and outward conductance at least
`1/[48|R_m|(m-2)M^2]`.  Prefix locality supplies an `O(1/m)` upper bound on
fiber gap, which is only polynomial slowness.  A general exit-propensity
construction refutes a naive mean-lift constant one, now architecturally moot

### L-0031 - Exact macro projection and square down-up comparison

For a feasible macro datum `alpha={(B,k_B)}`, with `C(v,k)` the number of
connected identity factorizations on a fixed labeled v-set, the coordinator
proved the exact fiber weight

    W_m(alpha)=m! product_B C(|B|,k_B)/k_B!,
    mu(alpha)=W_m(alpha)/|Omega(N,m)|.

The M02 artifact gives the exact stationary-flow transition formula by
averaging, for every `r=3,4,5` window, the number of same-product replacements
landing in each target macro divided by its exact block-fiber size.

It then defines `S`, the stationary-flow macro projection of refreshing the
first adjacent square uniformly and otherwise holding.  If `gamma` is a
length-`m-2` macro and `d_gamma(alpha)` counts transpositions whose square
insertion produces `alpha`, then for distinct macros

    S(alpha,beta)
      = [1/(M W_m(alpha))] sum_gamma W_{m-2}(gamma)
          d_gamma(alpha)d_gamma(beta).

Padding the square by the third factor uses a transposition-product triple
fiber of exact size

    D_N=(3N^2+N-12)/2 <= 4M.

Consequently the exact macro projection satisfies

    gap(bar_K)
      >= M/[2|R_m|(m-2)D_N] gap(S)
      >= gap(S)/[24(m-2)].

All source-core weights remain explicit.  Paired representatives show `S`
is irreducible and has diameter at most `m` through a repeated-edge macro, but
that hub route is not used for congestion.  The result is a quantitative
projection reduction, not a projection gap theorem, and is pending fresh
audit before promotion.

Artifacts:
`experiments/20260811-polynomial-mixing-resolution/cycle-02/mission-M02-coordinator/`.

Verdict: PROVED BY COORDINATOR, PENDING INDEPENDENT AUDIT.  First unproved
step: SQUARE-DOWN-UP-GAP.

### L-0032 - Aligned length-four bridge chain is qualitatively irreducible

Every adjacent pair of original transposition positions lies inside at least
one aligned window starting at an odd position.  Holding the other two
factors fixed therefore realizes both oriented Hurwitz rewrites and every
square refresh as actual outcomes of that aligned `H_4`.  The independently
audited classification-and-paired-representative proof for `K` consequently
applies verbatim to the nonlazy aligned chain `B`.

Thus `B` is irreducible for every `N>=3`, even `m>=4`; at `m=4` it is the
whole-word heat bath.  A specified move has probability at least
`1/[(m/2-1)M^3]`, which is used only for positivity.  No gap or congestion
claim follows.

Artifact:
`cycle-02/mission-M02-coordinator/aligned_r4_irreducibility.md`.

bridge Poincare proposition now has only a quantitative gap clause.

### L-0033 - M15 bridge-theorem and planar-map transfer audit return

The targeted continuation rigorously refuted three proposed theorem
transfers, without refuting the bridge chain or global goal.

1. Dyer--Goldberg--Jerrum's and Pedersen's path-colouring results fix the
   target graph.  Restoring their displayed dependence at
   `|A_N|=N!/2` gives superpolynomial blocks or congestion; their laws,
   endpoints, weights, and update kernels also differ from `B`.
2. Lyu--Memoli--Sivakoff's printed weighted-path contraction constant has
   incompatible max/min and reciprocal uses.  Under the natural worst-case
   repair, an exact feasible `N=5` boundary pair has
   `TV=4441/11730`, making the degree-two margin negative.  At `N=6` on nine
   interior sites, every standard dependency-matrix norm exceeds a Perron
   lower bound `30913/30600>1`.  These refute sufficient contraction
   hypotheses only.
3. Duchi's minimal-factorization bijection uses simple, face-labeled,
   black-indexed increasing quadrangulations.  Caraceni's polynomial-gap
   chain is uniform on all rooted unlabeled multigraph quadrangulations and
   intertwines through Tutte's different bijection.  No literal
   stationary-measure-preserving bounded simulation transfers.

Artifact:
`cycle-01/mission-M15/continuation-bridge-literature/report.md`, with exact
influence harness and rational output.

### L-0035 - Uniform dense total-surplus thresholds rejected

M12 proved that for `M=binom(N,2)`, every `N>=3`, even `m` satisfying

    m-2 >= 4M log(2N),

and every nontrivial total-surplus threshold `A_{<=g}`,

    Phi_pm(A_{<=g}) >= (N-1)/(128M^2).

The identity `G=m/2-N+c`, with isolated labels included in component count
`c`, turns these into component-count thresholds.  A direct-product central
character trace retaining every parity mode bounds every exact-support count
within a factor four.  Component-layer mass then has geometric ratio at most
`4/N^4`, proving that the disconnected upper tail is the smaller side.
Counting from that side, internal square deletion followed by an actual

### L-0037 - Nonnormal Hurwitz geometry isolated; polynomial diameter proved

M13 found no TERMINAL B obstruction, but rigorously removed every ordinary
normal-quotient explanation for a slow Hurwitz orbit.  For a project Hurwitz
orbit `B_m/H`, a paired representative is fixed by a standard braid
generator.  Thus `H` contains a conjugate of that generator; any homomorphism
whose kernel contains `H` kills every conjugate standard generator and is
trivial.  A more general hypothetical coordinate satisfying
`phi(R_i x)=phi(x)+1 mod q` has `q|m(m-1)` and exact lazy-cycle dynamics, with
gap at least `4/q^2` and full two-sided cut accounting.  In the project orbit
the stabilizer argument sharpens this to `q=1`.

An explicit counter audit of Ben-Itzhak--Teicher Lemmas 3.7--3.10 also proves

    dist_H(x,y) <= 50 N m^3

for two identity transposition words with identical labeled weighted support
components.  This is only a diameter theorem: routing through the canonical
paired word would recreate the known exponentially concentrated hub and no
congestion estimate is inferred.

At minimal full support, the join positions form a spanning tree and one
Hurwitz move changes at most one tree edge.  Exact enumeration simultaneously
refutes the tempting uniform-tree and exact Dyck/tree/degree/Pruefer-lumping
reductions.  The coordinator reran the deterministic harness and obtained a
byte-identical `results.json` for the recorded `S_3,m=4`, `S_4,m=4`, and
`S_4,m=6` checks.

### L-0042 - Universal connected-genus ratio and polynomial boundary action

M12 proved from Dubrovin--Yang--Zagier Proposition 1, equation (13), that for
every degree `d>=2` and genus `g>=1`, with
`k=2(d-1)+2g` and `a_{g,d}=C_g(d)/k!`,

    a_{g,d}/a_{g-1,d} <= d^3/12.

The proof is an induction in `d` using a nonnegative convolution-ratio lemma.
Each ordered degree split contributes a convolution whose three ratio
constants sum to at most
`(d_1^3+d_2^3+d_1^2)/12<=d^3/12`.  The cited cover/fixed-sheet normalization
differs by a genus-independent `d!`, so the ratio is unchanged.  The
coordinator inspected the full induction and reran the exact normalization
harness with no failure through `d<=10,k<=60`.

For every exact labeled support partition `P` with positive total surplus,
the component/color factorization then gives

    E_P(m)/E_P(m-2) <= m(m-1)N^3/12.

Counting every internal square insertion and every crossing-edge output in an
actual first-window `H_3` update, with exact `D_N` and no hidden `1/M`, yields
the unconditional boundary-layer averaged rate

    Q_K(c=c_0+1,c=c_0)/pi(c=c_0+1)
      >= 1/[N^4 m(m-1)(m-2)]

for `N>=3`, even `m>=2N`.  If the component-layer counts `W_c(N,m)` are
unimodal, an exact orientation lemma supplies another factor `1/N`, proving
the same polynomial two-sided conductance for every threshold.  Universal
unimodality remains open.  It is proved for all `N` at `m<=8` and for all even
`m` at `N<=4`; exact searches through `N<=22,m<=220` found no failure but are
nonproof.  A natural exponential-Riordan TP2 route is rigorously inapplicable
because its first relevant minor has `t^4` coefficient `-1/4`.

### L-0050 - M05 shuttle comparison reaches a recoverable polynomial flow

The M05 halfway construction compares the full macro Hurwitz walk with an
ideal product chain consisting of the fixed-content adjacent color shuffle
and independent lazy symmetric Hurwitz walks on the nontrivial components.
For an ideal internal generator whose consecutive same-color factors occur at
physical positions `u<v`, it commutes the left factor across the intervening
disjoint colors, applies the desired Hurwitz generator, and reverses the
commutations.  The route has length `2(v-u-1)+1<2m` and leaves the color word
unchanged at its endpoint.

The proposed congestion recovery uses metadata `(component,u,v,orientation,
stage)`.  For fixed metadata the route prefix is a fixed bijection, so a used
directed physical Hurwitz edge recovers its source by inversion; repeated
colors do not create an exponential multiplicity.  Fewer than `m^4` metadata
values, route length below `2m`, and directed base-edge capacity at least
`pi/[4(m-1)]` give the deliberately loose provisional congestion bound
`2m^6`.  Conditional on component Hurwitz gaps `gamma_a`, this would yield a
full macro-fiber gap polynomially comparable to
`delta=min(1,min_a gamma_a)` and, through the inherited full-`K` comparison,
a lower bound of order `delta/(N^4m^10)`.

This is a halfway checkpoint, not yet a promoted theorem.  The final audit
must verify the exact ideal-chain normalization, endpoint action for both
Hurwitz orientations, recovery at shuttle/central-edge collisions, trivial
components, and every calibration boundary.  No fixed color restriction or
canonical hub is used.

### L-0054 - Constant-loss bridge recursion above the relative-mixing scale

M10 proves a halfway functional-inequality theorem for the microfactorized
`Q=P_transposition^2` bridge.  Let `Lambda_N(l;u,v)` be the continuous-time
single-site heat-bath Hamiltonian gap for a fixed endpoint pair, and minimize
over feasible pairs.  There is `s_*(N)=O(N log(N+1))` for which

    ||A_N| Q^s(x,y)-1| <= 1/16

uniformly once `s>=s_*`.  The parity-paired Diaconis--Shahshahani character
sum, rather than their total-variation conclusion, supplies this relative
bound.

For two length-`L` blocks overlapping in `s in {s_*,s_*+1}` steps, arbitrary
endpoint tilts give outside-tail maximal correlation at most `64/225`.  The
principal-angle formula and interval Hamiltonian comparison therefore give

    Lambda_N(n) >= Lambda_N(L)/3.

Choosing `b_N=s_*+1` makes `L-b_N<=(n-b_N)/2`; iteration loses only the fixed
power `log_2 3`, not a scan factor at every scale.  The coordinator checked
the Markov-tail equality, projection angle, local-Hamiltonian coverage, and
the endpoint-tilt constant (the safe direct bound `66/225` also suffices).

The exact remaining implication is a uniform inverse-polynomial
`Lambda_N(l;u,v)` for every feasible fixed endpoint and
`2<=l<=s_*(N)+2`.  It includes qualitative irreducibility.  M10 also proves
that sublinear overlap cannot give a uniform constant angle by the standard
representation mode.  Neither the recursion nor that obstruction is a
terminal result.

### L-0062 - Arbitrary-product Hurwitz classification closes qualitative bridge connectivity

The resource audit identifies a direct primary theorem for every endpoint,
not only the identity.  Douvropoulos--Lewis, *Electronic Journal of
Combinatorics* 31(1) (2024), #P1.27, published Theorem 18, proves that two
reflection factorizations of a parabolic quasi-Coxeter element of a finite
Coxeter group are Hurwitz-equivalent exactly when they generate the same
subgroup and have the same multiset of subgroup-conjugacy classes.  Every
permutation in `S_N` is parabolic quasi-Coxeter: minimally factor each
disjoint cycle, obtaining `N-#cycles(g)` transpositions which generate the
corresponding Young parabolic.  The project convention differs only by
inverting braid generators.

In type A, the generated subgroup is the direct product of the symmetric
groups on the labeled support components, and its conjugacy-class multiset is
exactly the component-weight list.  Hence arbitrary-product transposition
Hurwitz orbits have precisely the same graph-component/weight classification
as in the identity case.  Every adjacent Hurwitz move is an outcome of an
aligned four-window heat bath.  Hurwitz exposure of bridge/surplus squares,
followed by exact square refreshes, connects the macro data; the minimum-
length case already has a unique cycle-component macro.  Thus every nonempty
even-length arbitrary-endpoint aligned-H4 fiber is qualitatively connected
for `N>=3` and transposition length at least four.

The coordinator checked the primary published PDF directly.  The owned note
uses arXiv-style labels `Theorem 4.2` and `Definition 2.4`; the published
PDF labels these `Theorem 18` and `Definition 5`.  This is a version-
numbering correction, not a mathematical change, and has been sent back to
the owner.

The same audit refutes property `(T)` of the universal sixth-power braid
quotient as an automatic gap route: already the three-strand quotient has a
Euclidean triangle-group quotient.  This does not refute property tau or a
direct polynomial gap for the particular finite Hurwitz actions.

Artifact:
`cycle-03/mission-M05-referee/post_referee_resource_note.md`.

Verdict: PROVED/CITED QUALITATIVE THEOREM.  Arbitrary-endpoint
irreducibility is removed from the M10 base obligation; its quantitative gap
is unchanged.

### L-0065 - Dense allocation curvature threshold improves to O(v log v)

The M07 refinement replaces the previous `Theta(v^2 log v)` connected-count
smoothing threshold.  Let `k=2(v-1)+2g`, `M_v=binom(v,2)`, and let `A` be
the universal parity-aware pointwise random-transposition constant inherited
from the checked Diaconis--Shahshahani partition-zone estimates.  With
`C_*=256(A+1)`, it proves

    k-2 >= C_* v log(v+1)
    implies rho_v(g+1)-rho_v(g) >= 3k/M_v^2.

probability before conditioning on the global identity return.  For a missing
cut of smaller side `q`, a binomial allocation and pointwise mixing on the
larger side cost only `v!/(v-q)!<=v^q`; a Chernoff remainder absorbs the full
factorial.  Uniform relative errors at `k-2,k,k+2` then give the stated
positive increment with all scalar constants explicit in `A`.

multiplicity, Chernoff threshold, adjacent-ratio propagation, and final
constant.  No computation is a dependency.

### L-0072 - Additive binary-collision theorems do not transfer directly

The post-referee resource audit checks Caputo's complete-graph binary-
collision theorem and Sasada's local-to-complete comparison at their exact
hypotheses.  Both condition an exchangeable product law on an additive
coordinate sum.  Their local comparison uses literal coordinate swaps and a
conditional-expectation conjugation identity.  An ordered noncommutative
bridge has neither property: an explicit positive-support triple in `A_4`
has product identity, while swapping its first two increments changes the
product.  Nonadjacent raw pair refreshes likewise fail to preserve the
ordered endpoint.

Thus neither theorem supplies a gap for the present chain; even the
complete-graph theorem would additionally require a uniform three-coordinate
gap strictly above `1/3`, not merely a positive inverse-polynomial value.
What survives is only a proposed noncommutative extension in which swaps are
replaced by braid/Hurwitz shuttles and their capacities are proved anew.

`cycle-04/mission-M10-referee/post_referee_resource_audit.md`.

as Terminal B.  The next structural audit moves to the exact local-projector
and parent-Hamiltonian algebra, where group covariance may replace ordinary
exchangeability.

### L-0075 - Exact block-normal parent Hamiltonian improves the long-length loss

The M15 resource lane and an independent MPS auditor identify the aligned
bridge Hamiltonian exactly as a block-normal matrix-product parent.  On the
micro alphabet `E=T_N^2`, the Peter--Weyl tensor

    C^e=M^-1 direct_sum_(rho in dual(A_N)) rho(p(e))

has length-`r` span equal to amplitudes that are functions of the ordered
product.  Its two-site support projector is literally the exact aligned
`H_4` heat bath; no multiplicity-changing similarity transform is used.
Arbitrary-product aligned connectivity makes this a true parent interaction,
and the full Hamiltonian decomposes by endpoints with

    Delta(H_l)=min_h Lambda_N(l;h).

The nontrivial overlap in Nachtergaele's martingale theorem is exactly the
worst endpoint-sector maximal correlation between the two raw tails of a
three-block bridge.  Parity-aware pointwise mixing of `Q=P_T^2` and the
endpoint-tilt lemma give an integer `R_N<=C N log(N+1)` with overlap at most

    Lambda_N(n)>=Lambda_N(R_N)/[4(R_N-1)].

### L-0079 - Canonical and synchronized defect buffers incur superpolynomial accounting loss

The Cycle-05 M09 comparison lane rigorously isolates why two proposed zipper
architectures cannot close the base bridge gap.  Write `E=T_N^2`,
`q=|E|=M^2`, and let `F_r(g)` be the length-`r` microfactor buffer fiber of
ordered product `g`.  If every randomized comparison route is forced through
one of `J` width-`r` cylinders whose codebooks have total size `S`, then some
feasible endpoint has cylinder mass at most `JS/q^r` and the directed
comparison congestion is at least

    q^r/(4JS).

After a pointwise-mixed complement the same statement holds for every
prescribed endpoint, including identity, with only the factor
`kappa=(1+epsilon)/(1-epsilon)`.  A universal defect buffer needs
`r>=floor((N-1)/2)`, so polynomially many representatives per defect give an
explicit superpolynomial lower bound on this architecture's congestion.

The obstruction persists for the natural lossless synchronized-complement
recovery even when both buffers range over their entire fibers.  For fixed
`C>1/2`, odd `N`, `(N-1)/2<=r<=CN`, and an `N`-cycle `c`, an exact cover bound
and Fourier positivity prove

    |F_r(e)|/|F_r(c)| >= (2/N) exp(a_C N-3C),
    a_C=exp(-16C)/(2C).

Thus the recovery metadata must be at least the square of this exponential
ratio.  On the other hand, a fully randomized balanced shift over every legal
buffer is exactly an all-pairs comparison of the whole-fiber heat bath to the
adjacent bridge chain on `Omega_{r+1}(s)`.  Polynomial congestion for that
object is therefore the missing base theorem itself, not a reduction of it.
Fractional flows remain a possible escape, and none of these lower bounds is
a slowdown theorem for the Markov chain.

### L-0082 - Exact complete-pair recursion has an O(N^4) correlation core

The coordinator proves a self-contained leave-one-coordinate theorem for the
complete-pair heat-bath operator

    C_(l,h)=binom(l,2)^(-1) sum_(i<j) P_ij

on the microfactor bridge `E=T_N^2`.  Conditional on `X_k=e`, with
`g=p(e)`, deleting site `k` and simultaneously conjugating every later
microfactor by `g` maps the conditional law bijectively to the shorter bridge
of endpoint `h g^(-1)` and intertwines every pair heat bath avoiding `k`,
including pairs straddling the deleted site.  Exact pair-counting then gives

    gap(C_(l,h)) >= gamma_(l-1)^* [1-mu_l(h)],

where `gamma_(l-1)^*` is the worst shorter-endpoint complete-pair gap and
`mu_l(h)` is the top mean-zero eigenvalue of
`A_l=l^(-1)sum_k E[.|X_k]`.

Centrality of `Q=P_T^2` makes every ordered two-coordinate marginal identical
for `i<j`.  The nonzero spectrum of `A_l` is therefore the spectrum of an
`l`-by-`l` Gram matrix with identity diagonal, one forward correlation
operator `K` above the diagonal, and `K*` below it.  All product-fiber-zero
functions lie in `ker K intersect ker K*`; the entire off-diagonal range is
carried by products in

    {id, 3-cycles, double transpositions},

of cardinality `1+2 binom(N,3)+3 binom(N,4)=O(N^4)`.  This avoids the
factorial Peter--Weyl bond dimension but is not itself a gap bound: `K` is
generally nonnormal for noncentral endpoints, and a polynomial loss at each
level would still multiply superpolynomially.

`cycle-05/mission-M10-parent-hamiltonian/cgl_complete_chain_checkpoint.md`.

Verdict: COORDINATOR-PROVED STRUCTURAL REDUCTION, INDEPENDENT AUDIT ACTIVE.
The first unproved proposition is COORDINATE-CORRELATION ACCUMULATION: bound
the product of `1-mu_l^*` through `O(N log N)` by one inverse polynomial, or
replace the crude recursion with an equally quantitative CGL-style estimate.

### L-0083 - Correction to L-0082: cyclic covariance makes the correlation normal

The sentence in L-0082 describing the forward correlation operator as
generally nonnormal is superseded.  Although the ordered pair law is not
exchangeable, the bridge is invariant under the endpoint-twisted cyclic braid

    (X_1,...,X_l)
      ->(X_2,...,X_l,h^(-1)X_1h).

Together with position-independence of ordered pair marginals, this gives
`(X,Y)` equal in law to `(Y,h^(-1)Xh)`.  If `U_h f=f(h^(-1)(.)h)`, the forward
correlation satisfies

    K=K* U_h,    K*=U_h^(-1)K,    KU_h=U_hK.

Hence `K` is normal.  Jointly diagonalizing `K` and `U_h` reduces each block
of the coordinate Gram matrix to the scalar Hermitian matrix with diagonal
one, constant upper entries `kappa`, and constant lower entries
`conj(kappa)`.  For real `kappa` its unnormalized spectrum is

    1+(l-1)kappa,  and  1-kappa (multiplicity l-1).

For nonreal `kappa`, the eigenvalues are

    1+(z kappa-conj(kappa))/(1-z),
    z^l=conj(kappa)/kappa.

All are divided by `l` in the coordinate-retention operator.  The normality
and scalar formulas are proved algebraically in the amended coordinator
checkpoint and are under independent audit.  They sharpen the frontier but
do not bound a dangerous positive real `kappa` away from one.

Verdict: PROVED COORDINATOR CORRECTION, INDEPENDENT AUDIT ACTIVE.  The named
gap is now SCALAR CORRELATION ACCUMULATION.

### L-0123 - The ideal parking chain reduces to adjacent-value moves

### L-0113 - Lorentzian ideal chain closes the parking-macro comparison source

The coordinator proves an exact polynomial-gap ideal chain on the parking
count-vector state space

    J_r={m in N^r: sum_i m_i=r,
                   sum_{i=0}^k m_i>=k+1 for every k<r}.

Its stationary law is

    pi_r(m)=[r!/(r+1)^(r-1)] product_i 1/m_i!.

The support `J_r` is M-convex by an explicit last-tight/first-tight symmetric
exchange proof. Therefore its normalized support polynomial is Lorentzian.
Polarizing every type into `r` clones preserves Lorentzianity, and the
strongly-log-concave down-up theorem gives clone-chain gap at least `1/r`.
Lumping the clones by occupancies preserves that lower bound. Exact clone
multiplicity cancellation identifies the projected chain: choose a source
ball uniformly, delete it, then choose uniformly among the types whose
reinsertion is feasible. Every positive off-diagonal transition has
probability at least `1/r^2`, and one step is polynomial-time.

This is the first exact polynomial comparison source with precisely the
parking macro stationary law. It does not compare itself to the actual
projected rank-two heat bath. The standalone missing proposition is
LORENTZIAN-TO-HEAT-BATH PARKING FLOW: a polynomial-congestion fractional
routing of all ideal unit exchanges through actual positive macro edges.

Dependencies checked in the coordinator report are Branden--Huh, Annals of
Mathematics 192 (2020), Proposition 3.1 and Theorem 3.10, and
Anari--Liu--Oveis Gharan--Vinzant, Theorem 1.1. This result still awaits a
fresh independent referee.

### L-0138 - Exact Cayley lift of the parking-macro law

Append a terminal zero to a parking multiplicity vector `d`. Its prefix
conditions are exactly the Lukasiewicz conditions for one rooted plane tree
with preorder outdegrees `d`. The parking stationary law is

    pi(d)=r!/[(r+1)^(r-1) product_i d_i!].

This law is exactly the pushforward of the uniform Cayley tree on labelled
vertices `{0,...,r}`, rooted at zero: order every vertex's children by their
numeric labels, take preorder outdegrees, and forget labels. For a fixed
plane shape, the sibling groups partition the nonroot positions, so precisely
`r!/product_i d_i!` label assignments increase within every sibling group.

This identifies `pi` as the simply-generated, conditioned-Poisson(1) plane
tree law. It also rigorously rejects a direct use of Caraceni--Stauffer's
uniform-plane-tree leaf-replanting/translation gap: star and path densities
differ here by `r!`, and their move is not the parking sibling-subtree
rotation. A labelled-tree randomized comparison remains possible but must
preserve stationary flow. For a leaf rotation `d->d-e_p+e_j`, the exact mass
ratio is `d_p`, matching the candidate forward/reverse rate ratio.

Artifact:
`cycle-09/mission-M41-adjacent-parking-flow/checkpoint.md`, Section 6.

Verdict: PROVED REPRESENTATION AND HYPOTHESIS MISMATCH; GAP OPEN.

### L-0143 - Weighted leaf-replanting gap proved and freshly accepted

For rooted plane trees with `r` edges and stationary law

    pi_r(t) proportional to product_v 1/d_v(t)!,

define the lazy Metropolis chain `Y_r` on root-preserving leaf replantings
with off-diagonal rate

    (4r^2)^(-1) min(1,pi_r(t')/pi_r(t)).

The coordinator proves

    gap(Y_r) >= 1/[16(r+1)^8].

Fleurat's time-reversed growth coupling is used only through its exact row
sum and aggregate column balance.  Independent deletion histories from a
shared pivot give intermediate load
`pi_(i-1)(L)pi_(r-i)(R)`.  With `N=r+1`, its exact density ratio is

    k Z_r/[Z_(a-1)Z_(b-1)] = kab/[N p_(N,a)],

where `p_(N,a)` is a binomial mass at its mean; this is at most `N^3`.
Every replant changes the stationary density by a factor in `[1/N,N]`, so
capacity is at least `pi(t)/(4N^3)` and the full path congestion is at most
`16N^8`.

M46 independently checks the literal replanting assembly, shared-pivot
marginal, partition-function identity, endpoints, neighbor count, laziness,
capacity, and congestion, and returns **ACCEPT** with no substantive
objection.  Its only editorial requests are to name the comparison source as
the one-step global heat bath, suppress null replant steps, and state that
the two pivot-conditioned deletion histories are independent.

### L-0146 - Exact maximum-degree separator mass for parking trees

The coordinator proves an exact stationary-law reduction for the emerging
reversible-pebbling obstruction.  By the cycle lemma, the conditioned-
Poisson parking degree vector is the Vervaat shift of `r` independent balls
in `r+1` boxes.  Maximum occupancy is cyclically invariant, so for every
`D>=1`,

    pi_r(max_i d_i>=D)<=(r+1)(e/D)^D.

At `D_r=2+ceil(log_2(r-1))` this is smaller than every inverse polynomial.
If the low-degree leaf-rotation graph has a union of connected components
whose two sides both have inverse-polynomial stationary mass, then every
boundary edge enters this superpolynomially rare maximum-degree set and

    gap(L_r)<=pi_r(max d_i>=D_r)/[pi_r(A)pi_r(A^c)].

Thus a two-part component/mass theorem at the barrier threshold would
rigorously refute polynomial comparison from the accepted replanting chain.
The component separation and mass balance are still open.  Even a resulting
superpolynomially small auxiliary `L_r` gap would remain nonterminal until
the cut survives all other parking-projection moves and the full original
chain.

product of reciprocal outdegree factorials, let L_r retain exactly the

configurations on a path. Below maximum degree D_r there are at most
ceil(log_2(r-1)) pebbles, one fewer than the persistent reversible pebbling
number needed to connect the empty configuration to the sink singleton.
The corresponding union A_r of low-degree components has

    pi(A_r)>=e^-1-b_r,
    pi(A_r^c)>=3/(8e^2)-b_r,
    Q_L(A_r,A_r^c)<=b_r.

The two constant-mass reservoirs are respectively root-unary trees and
trees with ordered root branch sizes (r-1,1). The cycle-lemma occupancy
bound makes b_r smaller than every inverse polynomial. Hence

    gap(L_r)
      <=b_r/[(e^-1-b_r)(3/(8e^2)-b_r)]

and r^C gap(L_r) tends to zero for every fixed C. Since the accepted
weighted leaf-replanting chain has inverse-polynomial gap, every

### L-0153 - Full Pbar root-degree projection has a polynomial gap

Let d_0 be the root outdegree of the plane tree associated to the minimal
long-cycle parking macro with r edges. M35's Cayley pushforward gives

    d_0-1 ~ Bin(r-1,1/(r+1)).

For a function F(d)=f(d_0), the full projected rank-two Dirichlet form
contains every root-lowering aggregate transition. Summing each reversible
edge in its lowering orientation gives

    E_Pbar(F,F)
      >=sum_(k=2)^r Pr(d_0=k)
          [2k(k-1)/(3r(r-1))][f(k)-f(k-1)]^2.

The random-scan heat bath on r-1 independent Bernoulli variables with
parameter 1/(r+1) has gap 1/(r-1) and downward count rate
b(1-p)/(r-1). The coefficient ratio is at least
4(r+1)/(3r^2). Therefore the exact stationary-flow projection onto root
degree satisfies

    gap(Pbar projected to d_0)
      >=4(r+1)/[3r^2(r-1)].

At r=2 the bound equals one and matches the exact rates 2/3 downward and
1/3 upward. M46 independently audits the Cayley marginal, the absence of a
spurious factor 1/2, the Bernoulli comparison, and the boundary case, and
returns ACCEPT with no objection.

### L-0154 - Cyclic conjugation is not a parking-macro symmetry

The exact cycle-lemma occupancy representation does not permit a hidden
rerooting shortcut for the full parking projection.  Let a minimal
long-cycle micro factor tree be (G), let

    epsilon_a=1_{{a,r} in E(G)},

and conjugate every transposition by the long cycle, so labels increase by
one modulo (r+1).  If (d) is the source parking degree vector, the
conjugated target satisfies exactly

    d'_0=sum_(a=0)^(r-1) epsilon_a,
    d'_j=d_(j-1)-epsilon_(j-1),  1<=j<=r-1.

Thus the target depends on the incidence vector of the last label, which is
not determined by (d).  The smallest exact mismatch is already (r=2):
the same source macro (d=(1,1)) can conjugate to ((1,1)) or ((2,0)),
so even the target degree histogram is not determined.  At (r=3),
conditional coordinate exchangeability within one target histogram also
fails: from source ((1,1,1)), targets ((2,0,1)) and ((2,1,0)) occur
with different microstate multiplicities.

Long-cycle conjugation remains a global measure-preserving microstate
bijection, but it is not lumpable to degree vectors or histograms and cannot
justify exchangeable aggregate destinations.  A valid full-parking flow must
retain the incidence data or average at the micro level.

### L-0161 - Full-parking leaf-count thresholds have polynomial boundary flow

M46 freezes an exact anti-bottleneck theorem for the full parking projection.
Let `L` be the number of zero-outdegree vertices of the rooted plane tree.

that increases `L` by one at rate `2/[3r(r-1)]`, and deterministically
`U_*>=r+1-2L`.  Under `pi_r`, `L` is the number of empty boxes when `r` balls
are thrown into `r+1` boxes.  Its law is log-concave.  Analytic estimates for
`r>=128`, plus exact integer closure for `2<=r<=127`, imply that every
threshold whose two sides each have mass at least `1/4` has stationary flow
at least

    1/[60(r+1)(r-1)].

### L-0195 - Exact contextual means refute the proposed absorption lemma

M65 proves a closed formula for every contextual suffix-split conditional
mean in the full nonlazy parking projection.  If the outer root has `K`
branches, `R` lie to the right of the split branch, the inner root
composition has `k` parts and last part `t`, and the marked suffix has size
`B`, then after dividing by the common action rate the mean is

```text
0,                                                       t<B,
K binom(k+K-R-2,K-R-1)/binom(k+K-1,K-1),                t=B,
[w_(t-B)w_B/w_t]
 [1-binom(K,R+1)/binom(K+k,R+1)],                       t>B.
```

The proof deletes every factor except two ordered attachment chains and
counts their uniform binary shuffles; the terminal and deep cases are
separate exact marked insertion/deletion bijections.  The proof artifact
`closed_mean_formula.md` has SHA-256
`864287d4c7f8fe4e5177984b46d51acaf8dcd9a87f20647e66909dbad82b513a`.

This formula gives a symbolic infinite refutation of M56's proposed
CONTEXTUAL QUOTIENT ABSORPTION.  Take local rank `s=2`, suffix `B=1`,
outer context `R=K-1`, and the feasible ambient composition
`(3,1,...,1)`.  The two local compositions `(2)` and `(1,1)` have masses
`2/3,1/3`, normalized transition rates `1,2`, and conditional means

```text
q(2)=K/(K+1),       q(1,1)=2/(K+1),       E q=2/3.
```

Therefore

```text
||q-Eq||_(-1)^2=2(K-2)^2/[27(K+1)^2],
E[q 1_(last=B)]=2/[3(K+1)],
ratio=(K-2)^2/[9(K+1)].
```

The ratio first exceeds one at `K=14`, where it is `16/15`.  Equivalently
`delta+a=226/225`; it tends to `10/9`.  This is a rigorous refutation of an
auxiliary parking-flow lemma, not a slow mode or a Terminal-B result for
`K`.

The counterexample identifies its own repair.  Its only deep state recurses
to rank one, and universally a deep contextual step enters the last inner
branch with new right-context parameter `R=0`; the bad `R=K-1` geometry
cannot repeat.  Exact formula-based solves find no `R=0` absorption failure
through `s=11,K=100`, but that is finite evidence only.  The new standalone
steps are R=0 CONTEXTUAL ABSORPTION, a TERMINAL-AWARE CONTEXT RECURRENCE,

### L-0182 - Every subset of the minimal full-support macro has polynomial escape

M60 freezes a substantially stronger literal full-chain theorem at
`m=2N-2`.  Write `F_N` for the minimal connected full-support macro,

measurability assumption,

```text
Q_K(A,A^c)/pi(A)
 >= 1/[2 B N(N-1) Lambda_N (m-2) M^3]
 >= 1/(24N^13).
```

The proof partitions each fixed-tree fibre into cells indexed by a selected
leaf `v` and the word obtained by deleting its two null increments.  Every
cell has size at most `Lambda_N`.  Four explicit Hurwitz/square identities,
each realized inside a genuine padded `H_3`, send every cell state in at most

the identity product, and membership in `F_N` (the intermediate join tree may
change).  Every used positive transition has probability at least

```text
p_* = 1/[2 B (m-2) M^3].
```

If the hub is in `A`, one specified `H_3` square refresh exits `F_N`; if it is
not, assign each source its first path edge leaving `A`.  A directed edge can
be assigned at most `N(N-1)Lambda_N` sources.  Because only the single first
crossing is charged per source, this boundary-flow argument has no additional
path-length factor.  The same lower bound is a two-sided conductance bound,
since `min(pi(A),pi(A^c))<=pi(A)`.

### L-0184 - Root-split bias localizes exactly to the split branch

M56 proves the previously empirical CONTEXTUAL SPLIT LOCALITY statement.

depends only on the local macro `u_i` of the split branch (besides the stated

```text
||ell_(b,a)||_{-1,b}^2 / q_(b,a)
 = alpha_s ||phi-E phi||_{-1,Pbar_s}^2 / E phi,
```

because `alpha_r/gamma_i=alpha_s`.  This removes every ambient-`r`
normalization loss from the H-minus-one reduction.  Exact proportionality to

cannot be discarded.

### L-0168 - Stationary path lengths improve the root quotient to order r^-2

Using M50's exact root-composition formulas, the coordinator sharpens its
canonical-flow calculation.  For a merge edge whose refined endpoint has
`k` root branches, the source-basin mass is at most `e` times the endpoint
mass.  More precisely, Lagrange inversion and

    k!(p+k-2)/(p+k-1)! <= k/p!

show that the basin's path-length first moment is at most `ek` times endpoint
mass.  Since root degree minus one has exact mean `(r-1)/(r+1)<1`, both path
halves contribute at most `3ek` times endpoint mass.  The edge capacity is
`alpha_r k` times that mass, with `alpha_r=2/[3r(r-1)]`.  Therefore

    gap(root-composition quotient)
      >=alpha_r/(3e)=2/[9e r(r-1)].

This removes the extraneous factor `r` from M50's worst-length bound.  The
full calculation, including repeated-edge occurrence accounting and `r=2`,
is M41 checkpoint Section 11 at SHA-256
`891eb088411476677464a7a2a504d5d179201dc8b888d3875f8d47f99d3df5aa`.
It depends on M50's exact quotient formulas, currently under the independent
M54 audit.  It still does not bound the full parking gap without BALANCED
ROOT MEAN-LIFT.

Verdict: COORDINATOR PROOF FROZEN; EXACT DEPENDENCY/FRESH AUDIT PENDING;
NONTERMINAL.

### L-0175 - M58 freezes all join-tree-measurable cuts at minimal length

Let `N>=4,m=2N-2`, let `F_N` be the minimal connected full-support macro,

isomorphism or relabeling invariance.  Put

    R_A={w in F_N:T_J(w) in A}.

`F_N^c union R_A` and `F_N^c union (F_N\R_A)` satisfy

    Phi_pm >=1/[3Nm(N-1)^2(m-2)D_N] >=1/(24N^7).

For each possible deleted leaf label `v`, let `P_v(A)` be the pairs
`(base,u)` for which attaching `v` to `u` creates a tree in `A`.  Strand
deletion, now recording the old neighbor, gives

    |R_A| <= binom(m,2)(N-1)^2 sum_v |P_v(A)|.

Conversely every valid pair and every insertion gap creates an adjacent-
square source.  There is at most one representation per label and hence at
most `N` representations per source, so at least

    (m-1)sum_v |P_v(A)|/N

distinct sources exist.  Selecting one representation per source prevents
flow overcounting.  One genuine padded `H_3` outcome exits to `F_N^c` with
the exact factor `1/[6(m-2)D_N]`; all other `H_3,H_4,H_5` terms remain
nonnegative.  Repeating the argument for `A^c` and using exact global masses
handles both orientations.  The theorem subsumes all earlier scalar,
quadrant, and rectangle join-tree profile cuts.

### L-0192 - Contextual spine bias has an exact additive marked recurrence

M56 freezes the next constructive reduction for the full nonlazy parking
projection `Pbar_r`. For every contextual root split, its target propensity
depends only on the local macro of the split branch. If that branch has
internal size `s`, the exact dual-norm normalization satisfies

```text
||ell||_(-1,b)^2/q
 =||p_s^ctx-E p_s^ctx||_(-1,Pbar_s)^2/E p_s^ctx;
```

the ambient action fraction cancels because
`alpha_r/gamma_(r,s)=alpha_s`. Conditional on the next root composition,
the contextual suffix statistic is zero when the last branch is too small,
constant on the equality fibre, and otherwise recurses into that unique
strictly smaller last branch. Thus the recursion never branches over all
children.

With separate capacity marks for quotient-edge residual commodities, M56
derives the exact energy recurrence

```text
T_s(f) <= a(f)+[delta(f)+a(f)] max_(t<s)T_t,
a(f)=||E[f|root composition]-Ef||_(-1,R_s)^2/Ef,
delta(f)=E[f 1_(last branch>B)]/Ef.
```

All restriction action coefficients telescope exactly. The remaining
scalar proposition is CONTEXTUAL QUOTIENT ABSORPTION,
`a(f)<=1-delta(f)`. Even after it, an actual-chain proof must recover the
active quotient-edge/depth history, or otherwise bound collisions among
marked residual flows additively. Exact solves through `r=7` give

```text
 [alpha_N|A|+p_min|L_(k+1)|]/min{|C_k|,|Omega|-|C_k|},
```

### L-0200 - First frozen long-cycle correlation proof rejected pending exact repair

An independent referee rejects frozen M61 theorem-note SHA-256
`2b3be8e7b645a9ea6065f83892ca22d93aae27e76c315bf5d946da435ec07f34`
for proof completeness, not because it found a false constant or a finite
counterexample.  The referee independently derives the audit-sensitive
`16n` disjoint-chord multiplicity and accepts the product-fiber conditional
expectation, contraction, and aligned Gram normalization

```text
1/l+(l-1) Re(kappa)/l.
```

The defect is that the frozen note only describes, rather than proves
uniformly, the shared-endpoint remainder.  The identified repair writes its
absolute contribution as

```text
16n (n-3)!/n^(n-2)
 sum_(a+b+c=n) A_a A_b A_c
   sin^2(pi a/n) sin^2(pi b/n).
```

Partitioning by a largest gap at least `n/3`, together with the uniform
Stirling bound and the two sine zeros, makes the sum `O(e^n n^-2)`; the
factorial prefactor after removing `e^n` is `O(sqrt(n))`, yielding the needed
`O(n^-3/2)`.  M67 must state this calculation explicitly in a new immutable
proof and obtain a new independent verdict.  Until then the claimed
`Omega(l^-1/2)` product-correlation mode is not promoted to proved status.

Verdict: FROZEN PROOF REJECTED AS INCOMPLETE; EXACT REPAIR IDENTIFIED;
NONTERMINAL.

### L-0219 - Genus-one Hurwitz walk has an exact spectrum-preserving handle cut

```text
g(u)=[length(u)-d-cycles(eval(u))+2 support_components(u)]/2.
```

Appending a transposition changes `g` by zero or one.  Every full genus-one
word has unique forward and backward handle positions `h(w),b(w)` with
`b(w)<h(w)`.  At every cut `j` in the nonempty interval

bipartite support-partition incidence graph has one edge per cycle of the
cut permutation and is connected unicyclic.  Connectivity is exactly the
condition that the left/right support partitions have one-block join.

```text
tilde pi(w,j)=pi_d(w)/|J(w)|,
L((w,j),(w',j'))=P_d(w,w')/|J(w')|
```

This is an everywhere-defined coordinate decomposition, not a gap proof.

handle coordinate alone is neither a strong lump nor pointwise mobile: an
explicit family has fixed `h=3` under every one-step proposal for all

and vanishes on the accepted M92 tree-indexed unmarked family.

root-independent involution

```text

```

However, `tau_i` is not generally one element of the diagonal signed Hurwitz
action.  If the same signed braid acts on two sources `x,y`, package their
factors as a tuple in `S_d x S_d`; every Hurwitz generator preserves the
literal subgroup generated by these paired factors.  At `d=3,i=3`, M127 gives

```text

x'=((23),(23),(13),(13)),  y'=((13),(13),(12),(12)).    (L-0234.2)
```

The source paired factors generate the parity-diagonal subgroup of
`S_3 x S_3`, of order 18.  The target paired factors generate the graph of
conjugation by `(123)`, of order 6.  Hence no signed Hurwitz word of any
length sends both sources to their required targets.  This rigorously refutes
source-independent fixed-braid intertwining already at `d=3`; analogous exact
subgroup witnesses occur for every adjacent index at `d=3,4`.

The complete finite `d=3` action image has order 216 and contains none of the
three involutions `tau_i`.  Nevertheless source-dependent schedules repair
the finite case: every one-braid agreement set has size at most six, and
exact union dynamic programming proves that five fixed-schedule classes are
necessary and sufficient for each `i`.  This five-class statement is exact
finite only and supplies no asymptotic cover.

Let `G=S_d` act on the minimal connected identity sector `Z_d` by global
conjugation and let `H=Stab_G(d)`.  The adjacent DPS value edit `tau_i`
commutes with `H`.  Both actions are free for `d>=3`, and every `G`-orbit

edges are `H`-orbits and whose endpoints are their source and `tau_i` target
`G`-orbits is therefore `d`-regular, hence decomposes into `d` perfect
matchings.

classification gives the exact paired-source dichotomy

```text

P_d={(p,q):sgn(p)=sgn(q)}.                            (L-0238.1)
```

exceptional outer automorphism of `S_6` is excluded because it sends the
transposition class to the triple-transposition class.  Each perfect matching
is consequently conflict-independent.  Thus

```text

chi(C_(d,i))<=d^6 for every d>=2,                    (L-0238.2)
```

where rank four uses 2,880 singleton classes and `2880<4^6`.

Conflict independence is only necessary for one fixed braid, not sufficient.

braid action is six, and the exact schedule-cover number is five.  Hence the
remaining theorem is POLYNOMIAL MATCHING-COSET REFINEMENT: refine the matching

and admit a polynomial-length representative.

### L-0246 - The natural reduced Pin lifting descriptor is identically trivial

M150 freezes report SHA-256

Its target is only the natural reduced Pin class-lifting invariant of aligned
transposition-tuple factorizations.

Let

```text
P_(d,s)={(g_1,...,g_s) in S_d^s: all coordinate signs agree}
```

and let `C_(d,s)` be its single conjugacy class of all transposition tuples.
The Euclidean Clifford root `a_(ij)=(e_i-e_j)/sqrt(2)` gives an explicit Pin
extension of `S_d`; the coordinate product restricted over `P_(d,s)` has
central kernel `Z=<z_1,...,z_s>`.  Quotient `Z` by the subgroup `K_C`
generated by lift commutators with the centralizer of a base class element.
The resulting class lift is conjugacy-equivariant, so the product of lifted
factors is preserved exactly by both signed Hurwitz directions.

For every `d>=4`, this reduced kernel is already trivial.  Fix coordinate
`q`, choose a transposition `v_q` disjoint from the base transposition `t_q`,
and let `h^(q)` have coordinate `t_qv_q` at `q` and identity elsewhere.
This is an all-even element of `P_(d,s)` and centralizes the base tuple.  If
`a,b` lift `t_q,v_q`, then orthogonality gives `ab=-ba` and

```text
[a,ab]=baba=-1=z_q.                                 (L-0246.1)
```

Thus every `z_q` lies in `K_C`, so `K_C=Z`.  The localized all-even witness
is essential: an all-odd centralizer would kill at most a diagonal relation.

At `d=3`, the two simple `A_2` root lifts satisfy the Coxeter relations
exactly and define a homomorphic section `S_3->S~_3`.  Hence every product-one
aligned sequence has identity lifted product.  Any central translate also
cancels because the relevant factorization length is even.  Therefore the
descriptor is constant at every rank, not merely on the tested matchings.

and finite-group implementation confirms two-point Pin fibers, the unique

Its scope is the balanced strict endpoint-sharing family in the nonlazy
minimal-long-cycle cyclic rank-two quotient, not the full bridge chain.

Let `z_k` be the complete signed two-step stationary score for the balanced
triangle source against its strict-edge double neighbour at `n=3k`.  For a
separated double-target encoding `(a,y,b,n-a-b-y;S)`, average `k^2|z_k|`
over `y` and call the result `A_k(a,b)`.  Keeping every intermediate colour,
triangle, collision, and lower-support term inside `z_k` before the absolute
value, the exact stationary cell weight is

```text
W_n(a,b)=(n-a-b-1) Gamma_n b_a b_b b_(n-a-b)/2,      (L-0248.1)
k^2 E_pi[|z_k| 1_D]=2 sum_(a,b) W_n(a,b)A_k(a,b).    (L-0248.2)
```

The weights sum to `(n-3)/(2n)`.  After normalization they converge in total
variation to two independent critical-Borel variables; in particular, for
fixed `a,b`, `W_n(a,b)->b_a b_b/2`.  Their exact critical moment is

```text
H_D=sum_(a,b<=D)b_a b_b min(a,b)
   =(2/pi)log D+O(1).                                (L-0248.3)
```

Consequently, if one common `lambda>0` satisfies

`a,b<=k^theta` yields `TV>=c log(k)/k^2`.  This is a rigorous conditional
refutation route, not a proof that the score has that growth.  Conversely,
the exact positive target is uniform boundedness of the triangle contribution
and the weighted sum in (L-0248.2).  A weaker `O(log k/k^2)` all-edge theorem
would still retain polynomial decay in the H0 route.

For any product-one group factorization `X=(x_1,...,x_m)`, the chronological

```text
R_(m-1),...,R_1,R_1,...,R_(m-1)                    (L-0249.1)
```

sends `X` to its simultaneous conjugate by `x_m`.  Moving any original

transport realizes conjugation by that factor in at most `4m-4` moves.
Therefore conjugation by an element of factor-word length `ell` costs at most
`(4m-4)ell`.  Literal Hurwitz orbit and inner Nielsen orbit coincide on
factorizations generating the chosen group, but generation alone gives no
polynomial bound on `ell`.

For an aligned `d>=5` M135 matching subfamily, accepted M141/M143 makes the
generated group the common-parity subgroup `P_(d,s)`.  On the coarser
coordinatewise absolute quotient `Q_d=Z_d/S_d`, a quotient braid has unique
coordinate transporters.  It lifts to a literal common braid exactly when
their parity vector is diagonal, equivalently its relative voltage in
`F_2^s/Delta` is zero.  If the quotient braid has length `L` and the remaining
transporter has aligned word length `ell`, the explicit lift costs at most

```text
L+(4m-4)ell.                                         (L-0249.2)
```

Low ranks show both why this distinction matters and where the exceptional
geometry lies.  At `d=3`, all `432` generating ordered pairs split into two
literal orbits of size `216`, exactly by relative parity framing.  At `d=4`,
the quotient action has `40` blocks of size three, precisely the fibers of

rank-four common-braid witness.  At capped `d=5`, the degree-`8400` quotient
action is transitive and primitive, but neither its group order nor an
asymptotic image theorem is claimed.

On the quotient-tuple point stabilizer `B_q`, `nu` is a homomorphism with

voltage in exactly the affine coset

```text
{nu(b,q):bq=r}=nu(b_0,q)+V_stab(q).                (L-0253.1)
```

Thus a zero-voltage lift exists exactly when this coset contains zero.

two physical endpoint lifts translate the route coset.  Zero-membership is
therefore asserted only for fixed physical endpoints (or matched simultaneous
relabeling).  Accepted inner common-parity loops have zero relative voltage;
they remove a zero-voltage literal transporter but cannot adjust a nonzero
coset and have no proved polynomial word length.

stabilizer voltages are zero, but the source and target are already separated
by the `S_4/V_4` quotient block invariant, so no route coset exists.  The

### L-0254 - First-mode leakage defeats scalar Jacobi induction, but terminal Cayley weights sum it

M167 freezes report SHA-256
`d18ed32964935b0a58f236b04464be847fe673d24bb37ad3c11b409ba78f0997`,
operator-theorem SHA-256
`ba552a74790df708d06e94066dfe3aa784c5c4eae5eb3e602490f2e3999ac627`,
and manifest SHA-256
`4e7dd6a68de760d07e6ba1202ff3699210170eb8260d797b505bd55e200df4ec`.
Its target is only the M162/M165 first-Jacobi upper bound for the normalized
auxiliary unweighted reset excursion.

For `mu_b=Beta(1,b)`, let `Q_b` remove the constant and first centered modes.
Take the degree-one retained observation `a=0`, whose likelihood is `1-u`,
and the child pure first mode

```text
f(u)=u-1/(b+2).
```

Although `Q_(b+1)(f)=0`, its normalized parent lift
`g=(1-u)f/sqrt(b/(b+1))` has a strictly positive quadratic tail, with exact
unit-child leakage

```text
Q_b(g)/||f||^2=4/[(b+3)(b+4)].                    (L-0254.1)
```

Thus no induction retaining only scalar child `T_1` can dominate the exact
shared-observation transfer: child first-mode energy reappears in the parent
high tail.

There is also no degree-uniform branch-local charge of that tail to the
killed atom.  Under the fresh `Beta(1,1)` law, for degree `d` and retained
Bernstein coordinates `e_a=L_a/sqrt(pi_a)`, `a<d`, the exact residual is

```text
R_d=sum_(a<d) Q_1(e_a)
   =(d+1)/(2d+1)[4^d/binom(2d,d)-1]
     -d/(d+2)+3d^2/[(d+1)(d+2)^2]
   =sqrt(pi d)/2-3/2+O(d^(-1/2)).                 (L-0254.2)
```

The killed mass is only `1/(d+1)`, and even after subtracting retained
constant energy the high tail diverges as `Theta(sqrt d)`.  This is an
operator counterfamily, not an actual-history reset counterexample.

The exact terminal exterior coefficient, with `n=L-1` and `d=r+1`, is

```text
eta_(n,r)
 =r n^(n-r-1)(n+2)!/
   [(n-r)!(r+1)!(n+2)^(n+1)]
 <=r/(r+1)!.                                      (L-0254.3)
```

For fixed `r` it tends to `e^-2 r/(r+1)!`, so
`sum_r eta_(n,r)R_(r+1)` is uniformly finite.  The endpoint `r=n` in
(L-0254.3) requires the separate calculation
`eta_(n,n)=1/(n+2)^n<=n/(n+1)!`; it is not covered by the displayed product
estimate for `r<n`.  Hence the local square-root obstruction is factorially
summable after exact terminal Cayley aggregation and supplies no divergent
actual-history family.

Fix an intermediate rank-two product `s`, put `q=s^-1h`, and let its three
residual cycles have sizes `d_i`.  A polygon edge `E_r=(r,r+1)` is generic
exactly when `s(r+1)=r+1`, equivalently when it is the actual directed edge

edges and `a_i` adjacent generic-edge pairs, summing every allowed target
lag counts

```text

2g_i g_j                                          (cycles i,j).  (L-0255.1)
```

The corresponding completion sizes are `(1,1,d_i-2)` and

```text
 =n^(n-2)/(n F_T)
   sum_(s in X_n) a(s) S(s)/A_s
     [C_B(s)/A_B-C_A(s)/A_A].                    (L-0255.2)
```

No quotient matrix, absolute value, or continuum approximation occurs.

Its scope is qualitative literal lifting of the minimal connected DPS
quotient, not braid length.

Let `Z_d` be the physical connected product-one words and `Q_d=Z_d/S_d`.
The white-root cover is

```text
R_d=(Z_d x [d])/S_d ~= Z_d/Stab(d) -> Q_d.         (L-0256.1)
```

It has degree `d`.  If a quotient-stabilizer braid satisfies
`h.w=rho.w`, then its action on the root fiber in the physical
trivialization is exactly `rho^-1`; in particular its sign is the transporter
parity.  For width `s`, the relative signs of the simultaneous root
monodromies are therefore precisely the accepted stabilizer voltage
`V_stab<=F_2^s/Delta`.

Equivalently, use the orientation double cover `O_d=Z_d/A_d->Q_d`.  The
tuple relative-orientation cover is

```text
O_(d,s)=Z_d^s/P_(d,s) -> Q_d^s,                   (L-0256.2)
```

that is, the product orientation cover modulo the diagonal deck subgroup.
For fixed physical source and target tuples, the route-voltage coset contains
zero exactly when their orientation lifts lie in the same braid orbit in
(L-0256.2).  Independent endpoint relabelings translate this coset; only
fixed endpoints or matched simultaneous relabeling preserve the displayed
zero test.  Merely hitting a marked root does not determine the sign of its
root-fiber permutation.

For every nonempty accepted `d>=5` matching subfamily, let `K_mon(q)` be the
simultaneous white-root monodromy group of the quotient point stabilizer.
The accepted inner common-parity loops realize every element of
`P_(d,s)`.  Relative sign then gives the exact sequence

```text
1 -> P_(d,s) -> K_mon(q) -> V_stab(q) -> 1,
K_mon(q)=relative_sign^-1(V_stab(q)).              (L-0256.3)
```

Thus `V_stab` is exactly the remaining orientation quotient after all
accepted inner loops, rather than another unspecified conjugacy invariant.



`4e0eba4c484429985fea14de14957fc878773e72d82129706f2e228939421330` /
`cec16135a1f4f21b3fd4b8b17b7be6c31ba739b7fcb3191d4e9caf620c874835` /
`e1d54262506c19ec7634c74658f3db2408bc46da8903393ad5a7c6f78841d5ec` /
`b16858ec31079ef321d5e0130bc5289ba596dab83f905aa13beb4a491fa03f51`,
and manifest SHA-256
`18ef89e547f8b8d7ddfb7f8f292afc1a5e9a1ba9537deb6a1921a0d331c297f8`.

Fix `d>=5`, a tree `T` on `[d]` rooted at the leaf `d` with root edge
`{1,d}`, its extension `T^+=T+{1,d+1}`, and an ordinary inherited edit
`i=2v-3`.  With the accepted plus-mirror DPS closure, define

```text
N=F_d(T,s_i pi_d)(0,d)^2,
A=F_(d+1)(T^+,s_i pi_(d+1)),
x=v-1, y=d-1, z=0, n=d.                            (L-0289.1)
```

M240 isolates the exact unresolved **DPS Initial-Defect Lemma `(IDL)`**.
If the stopped parent path `P(v)=(v,p(v),...,1)` is not strictly decreasing,
IDL says `N=A`.  If it is decreasing, IDL fixes every discrepancy: an
initial cursor `(x,y)` versus `(x,n)` at position `2v-2`; for each larger
label `r`, either a direct-child square `(r-1,y)^2` versus `(r-1,n)^2` or
one common doubled parent edge disjoint from `{x,y,n}`; and the final four
positions

```text
N: (z,x),(z,y),(z,n),(z,n),
A: (z,y),(z,y),(z,x),(z,n).                        (L-0289.2)
```

Every other factor agrees literally.  This is a precise assertion about the
global DPS stack closure and is not supplied by ordinary group multiplication
or the finite `d=5,6,7` endpoint tables.

Conditional on IDL, M240 proves the arbitrary-rank ordinary-edit theorem.
Chronological square transport moves the two cursors across every larger
slot.  Direct-child inverse pairs conjugate both differing squares to the
same `(r-1,x)^2`; either sign transports a common disjoint square.  The
five-move carrier sends the terminal source four-block in (L-0289.2) to the
target block, and the reversed sign-negated scan returns the transformed
target to `A`.  Thus the accepted route-normal word

```text
Q(v,epsilon) B_d Q(v,epsilon)^-1
```

is correct for every open row assuming IDL, while the identity word is
correct for every closed row assuming IDL.  Its exact conditional length is

```text
4(d-v-1)+5 <= 4d-7.                                (L-0289.3)
```

M242 independently ACCEPTS this strict conditional ordinary-edit theorem
with zero material objection.  Its no-import checker verifies 441 positive
and 441 negative square identities, 5,040 direct-child equalizations, 10,080
disjoint-square transports, 840 carrier cases, 1,009 synthetic IDL routes
and length/sign boundaries, and 210 intended fixed-boundary overlap cases;
its normalized result digest is
`afe5f7135bcfee3f109b97411e4dbeeaf3f78b1100290ce174dceaf79b27c11b`.

### L-0309 - Per-channel sign is an exact owner-correlation inequality

### L-0310 - The signed channel-two scalar-Abel vector bound is proved

Fix odd `k>=3`, put `D=2k-1` and `R=2k-3`, and retain the exact M276/M279
channel-two disjoint laws.  With

```text
a_L(s)=1-gamma_L(s),       a_F(s)=1-gamma_F(s),

W_s=A_s/a_L(s),
```

the endpoint-bearing Abel vector collapses before any norm to

```text
E_(k-1)Phi_(k-1)^D
 +sum_(u=1)^(k-2)E_u(Phi_u^D-Phi_(u+1)^D)
 =sum_(s=1)^(k-1) A_s Phi_s^D,                    (L-0310.1)

E_(k-1)=-6(k-1)/[k(2k-1)(2k-3)].
```

M282 disintegrates every ordered disjoint support pair exactly.  For Borel
weights `b_r=e^-r r^(r-1)/r!`, the one-component coefficient is

```text

1<=x<r.                                             (L-0310.2)
```

It retains the second edge, all four cross pairs, both component choices,
both directions, phase, and every support collision.  The symmetric abstract
half-profile is

```text
c_d=A_d+sum_(s=1)^(k-1) W_s V_s(d),
V_s=Z_s+Z_(D-s),       Z_r(d)=U_r(d)+U_r(D-d).     (L-0310.3)
```

```text
B(d)=(D/R)b_d+8/R,       1<=d<=k-1.
```

Uniform Borel-ratio estimates, including `r=1,2`, the midpoint, the
active/inactive cutoff, reflected support, and both zero endpoints, prove

```text
TV(V_s-B)=O(1/R).                                  (L-0310.4)
```

Meanwhile the exact normalizer identity gives

```text
a_F/a_L-1=2(k-1)/[R(k-s)(D-s)a_L],
sum_s W_s=-sum_s q_s(a_F/a_L-1)=O(k^-2),
```

Thus the common profile is multiplied by `O(k^-2)` before norms, and the


### L-0311 - OCI is a signed positive-composition inequality, not PSD dominance

M285 starts from the exact M280/M283 channel laws and groups the normalized
ordered products by their supported product `s`.  The resulting centered
coefficients are

```text
beta_1(s)=sum_(e in T(L),f in T(M):ef=s)nu_L(e)nu_M(f)
 -1/2 sum_(C in {C_-,C_+})
       sum_(e in T(C),f in T(M):ef=s)nu_C(e)nu_M(f),

beta_2(s)=a(s)[F_(r-2)(s^-1 L)/F_r(L)
                  -F_(r-2)(s^-1 F)/F_r(F)],        (L-0311.1)
```

where `a(s)=3` for a triangle and `2` for a double.  The full two-cycle
completion denominator in channel two already contains the fair exterior
component choice; there is no additional `1/2`.  Both coefficient families
sum to zero.

Every triangle has exactly three positive cyclic-composition/anchor
representations.  Across the separated and nested double forms, every
supported double has exactly four.  Dividing by those multiplicities gives
the exact phase/composition law

```text
eta_j(F_ell)
 =1/3 sum_(r,g_1+g_2+g_3=n) beta_j(s_T(r,g))R_T(g,ell)
 +1/4 sum_(r,g_1+...+g_4=n)
    [beta_j(s_S(r,g))R_S(g,ell)
     +beta_j(s_N(r,g))R_N(g,ell)].                 (L-0311.2)
```

There is no extra factor `n`: the anchor is already one of the support
vertices, and the three/four inverse representations are exactly the stated
multiplicities.  Both intermediate kinds and the complete `IS/IN/X`
partition remain literal.

For a residual owner of size `d`, put

```text
a_d=tau(d-1)/tau(d),
b_d=tau(d-2)/tau(d),
delta_d=b_d-a_d^2.
```

If `A_s(x)` is the scalar owner field on generic phases, then every admissible
lag has the nonnegative decomposition

```text
F_ell(s)=sum_x A_s(x)A_s(x+ell)
 +sum_(i:d_i>=3)delta_(d_i)C_ii^s(ell),            (L-0311.3)
```

with `delta_d>=0` by log-convexity of `tau` and `delta_d=O(d^-2)`.  The
formal `delta_2=-1` term changes only lag zero because a size-two residual
owner has at most one generic phase; therefore (L-0311.3) equals the actual
response on every OCI lag `2<=ell<=n-2`.

Substitution of (L-0311.3) into (L-0311.2) gives the exact unresolved
gapped owner-correlation inequalities `(GOCI_j)`.  They are equivalent to
the accepted OCI statements on the admissible lag domain.  Statewise each
repaired response is a positive-semidefinite circular autocorrelation, but
the centered `beta_j` are signed.  PSD therefore need not survive their
mixture.

The sole predeclared exact `k=3` test proves this obstruction in channel two.
For

```text

```

the repaired channel-two signed circulant satisfies

```text
v^T M v=-310962953715709/12252303000000 <0.        (L-0311.4)
```

All twelve admissible channel-lag OCI values at the same rank are strictly
positive.  Thus (L-0311.4) refutes only canonical signed-PSD dominance; it is
not an OCI counterexample and makes no all-rank sign inference.

### L-0312 - Rank lifting composes inside charts, but charts do not cover a color

M284 separates the three types that had previously been conflated. At old
rank `d`, the same-rank endpoint fiber is the braid-word preimage

```text
Ktilde_Q={beta in B_(2d-2): beta(w_Q)=tau_i(w_Q)}.
```

For a valid rooted extension chart `T`, the accepted local correction
`c_(T,i)` lies in `B_(2d)` and starts at the old-edited padded word `N_T`:

```text
c_(T,i)(N_T)=A_T.                                 (L-0312.1)
```

It is not by itself a same-rank element of the upper endpoint fiber. If
`j_d:B_(2d-2)->B_(2d)` is the standard injection fixing the last two
strands, then the exact liftable fiber is

```text
L_(T,i)={j_d(beta);c_(T,i): beta in Ktilde_(Q_T)}
        subset Ktilde_(Q_T^+).                    (L-0312.2)
```

For any family sharing the same literal terminal word `c`, injectivity and
right cancellation give the exact word-level intersection identity

```text
intersection_T {j_d(beta);c: beta in Ktilde_(Q_T)}
 ={j_d(beta);c: beta in intersection_T Ktilde_(Q_T)}.  (L-0312.3)
```

Equation (L-0312.3) is before quotienting to finite action images; those
images can identify distinct braids and can have larger intersections.
The displayed representative, rather than an abstract finite action, also
carries the length information.

The accepted all-rank local table has at most two literal corrections for
each fixed `(d,i)`: an ordinary odd edit is either closed/identity or uses
the common open word with every optional nonchild sign chosen negative;
internal even edits are identity; the cross seam uses
`(R_(2d-5)^-1)^2`; and the last seam uses `R_(2d-2)^-1`. Hence an explicitly
extension-compatible lower cover with `q` common braid schedules lifts one
rank to at most `2q` displayed classes, with length increment at most
`4d-7`. This is a one-step conditional statement, not an iterated
polynomial bound.

M284 then proves that the chart clause cannot be discarded. For every
upper rank `D>=6`, with `e_r=(0,r)`, let

```text
U_D=e_1,e_1,e_2,e_2,...,e_(D-1),e_(D-1),
V_D=R_2(U_D)
   =e_1,e_2,e_2 e_1 e_2,e_2,e_3,e_3,...,
     e_(D-1),e_(D-1).                             (L-0312.4)
```

The word `V_D` has identity product, connected support, and minimal length
`2D-2`. Every unedited extension-chart source has equal factors in each
canonical consecutive pair, and global conjugation preserves equality at
fixed positions. The first two factors of `V_D` are unequal, so its entire
global orbit is chartless. Every M135 perfect-matching color nevertheless
contains exactly one H-edge over `[V_D]`. Thus charts plus local correction
type do not cover any complete matching color.

The obstruction does not lower-bound the number of genuine schedule classes
and does not refute a polynomial refinement with source-dependent base
routes. The first open routing lemma is a polynomial-cardinality base-route
signature cover containing every chartless left orbit and the actual H-edge
selected by an arbitrary color, with common translated embedded action
intersections admitting polynomial-length representatives.

### L-0313 - The full channel-two collision vector is now controlled

M289 returns to the exact open embedding vector left by M276/M279.  For odd
`k>=3`, put `D=2k-1`, `R=2k-3`, `j=(k-1)/2`, and retain the
unnormalized disjoint first-split mass

```text
qhat_s=q_s a_F(s),                 1<=s<=j.
```

If `Phi_s^D` and `Psi_s^D` are the literal conditioned B/A disjoint
double profiles with collision coordinates `Delta I_l`, the target is

```text

||sum_(s=1)^j qhat_s(Phi_s^D-Psi_s^D)||_1=O(1/k).  (L-0313.1)
```

The proof first forms raw ordered support-pair profiles `mathcal B_s` and
`mathcal A_s`.  Their exact masses are

```text
sum mathcal B_s=12a_L(s),    sum mathcal A_s=12a_F(s).  (L-0313.2)
```

They include the first-edge pair, the disjoint second-edge pair, all cross
pairs, both B seams, the marked and untouched A components, every cyclic
phase, and all support collisions.  No subprobability is renormalized.
Writing

```text
delta_s=a_F(s)/a_L(s)-1
       =2(k-1)/[R(k-s)(D-s)a_L(s)],
```

the complete pre-difference collision profile is exactly

```text
H=sum_s q_s[(a_F(s)/a_L(s))mathcal B_s-mathcal A_s]
 =H_0+sum_s q_s delta_s mathcal B_s.               (L-0313.3)
```

Since `delta_s a_L(s)=O(k^-2)`, the normalizer term has circular
variation `O(k^-2)`.

For

```text
K_R(r,x)=b_x b_(r-x)/(R b_r),
U_r(x)=1[x<=r-2](r-x-1)K_R(r,x)
       +4(r-1)/(rR)-2K_R(r,x),
Z_(M,r)(d)=U_r(d)+U_r(M-d),
```

M289 proves, uniformly for `M in {D,k}` and every component size including
`r=1,2`, the endpoint-inclusive stabilization

```text
TV_d[Z_(M,r)(d)-((r/R)b_d+4/R)]<=C/R.              (L-0313.4)
```

It also proves the exact two-seam embedding `mathcal E_D`, the one-wrap
embedding `mathcal E_k`, and a signed comparison lemma: whenever
`a_s=s w_s` has bounded endpoint-inclusive variation,

```text
TV(mathcal E_D f_w^D-mathcal E_k f_w^k)=O(1/k).   (L-0313.5)
```

The Borel tail and constant-tail extensions in (L-0313.5) are controlled
separately by their variation and l1 mass.  Applying (L-0313.4)--(L-0313.5)
to the first-edge, marked-component, and untouched-component rows leaves
only the explicit triangular cross profile, whose raw variation is `O(k)`
and whose coefficient is `O(k^-2)`.  Therefore `TV(H_0)=O(1/k)`.
Together with (L-0313.3), restriction of the adjacent differences to
`2<=l<=n-3` proves (L-0313.1).

### L-0314 - Individual chart frames are short; common chart prefixes are exponential

M291 fixes the doubled-star chart

```text
c_d=((1,2),(1,2),(1,3),(1,3),...,(1,d),(1,d))
```

in the minimal connected identity sector `Z_d`, `m=2d-2`.  For every
actual H-orbit edge `Q` selected by an arbitrary M135 perfect-matching
color, choose a source representative `w_Q` and target
`v_Q=tau_i(w_Q)`.  M13's accepted constructive Hurwitz diameter theorem
applies because `w_Q,v_Q,c_d` have the same single labeled weighted
component.  It gives literal source and target frames

```text
A_(alpha_Q)(w_Q)=c_d,       A_(delta_Q)(v_Q)=c_d,
|alpha_Q|,|delta_Q|<=50d(2d-2)^3.                  (L-0314.1)
```

Thus every selected edge separately has a route of length at most
`100d(2d-2)^3`.  If

```text
BStab_d={s in B_m:A_s(c_d)=c_d},
```

then its complete literal endpoint fiber is exactly

```text
Ktilde_Q=alpha_Q;BStab_d;delta_Q^-1.               (L-0314.2)
```

The right side is independent of the chosen normalizing frames.  A common
literal schedule for a family exists exactly when the corresponding fibers
in (L-0314.2) have nonempty intersection, and a polynomial-length
representative is an additional obligation.  At fixed rank, if `p_d` is
the finite action quotient, then

```text
p_d(intersection_Q Ktilde_Q)=intersection_Q p_d(Ktilde_Q),  (L-0314.3)
```

because one common finite action can be lifted to a braid acting
simultaneously on every source.  Equation (L-0314.3) supplies no shortest
representative and no cross-rank action embedding.

M291 next enlarges the allowed chart target to every ordered doubled Cayley
tree

```text
P_d={e_(sigma(1))^2...e_(sigma(d-1))^2},
|P_d|=d^(d-2)(d-1)!.                               (L-0314.4)
```

The physical left-endpoint set `C` of any complete matching color has

```text
|Z_d|=d^(d-3)(2d-2)!,       |C|=|Z_d|/d
    =d^(d-4)(2d-2)!.                                (L-0314.5)
```

A braid action is a bijection of `Z_d`, so one common prefix can send at
most `|P_d|` elements of `C` into (L-0314.4).  Every common-prefix
paired-chart cover therefore has

```text
q>=|C|/|P_d|
  =(2d-2)!/[d^2(d-1)!]
  >=d^(d-3).                                        (L-0314.6)
```

This is an arbitrary-rank counting theorem and already allows unlimited
prefix length.  It also applies to the actual H-edge formulation by
equivariance, and to common target suffixes by inversion.

The obstruction in (L-0314.6) is deliberately not a lower bound on genuine
common-schedule classes.  A full schedule `beta` requires

```text
alpha_Q^-1;beta;delta_Q in BStab_d
```

for every `Q`; the Q-dependent frames can cancel through different
stabilizer elements even when no common prefix reaches a paired chart.  The
first open routing lemma is therefore the exact framed-stabilizer
common-intersection problem: cover the intrinsic fibers (L-0314.2) by
`d^O(1)` families whose intersections contain literal `d^O(1)`-length
representatives, or prove this impossible.
