### L-0315 - Decorated owner response is exactly a small-owner signed tail

M290 starts from the literal residual-owner word `o_s(x)`, extended by a
null owner at nongeneric positions.  With `rho(0,·)=rho(·,0)=0`,
circular differentiation gives the exact endpoint law

```text
Delta Q_l(s)
 =sum_x[rho(o(x-l),o(x+1))-rho(o(x-l),o(x))].      (L-0315.1)
```

Thus only actual owner jumps contribute, while every phase, seam, owner
size, same-owner term, and cross-owner term remains literal.

Put

```text
a_d=tau(d-1)/tau(d),       b_d=tau(d-2)/tau(d),
u_d=e a_d-1,               delta_d=b_d-a_d^2,
G=sum_i chi_i,             B=1-G,
U=sum_i u_(d_i)chi_i.
```

Coefficientwise expansion of the exact owner kernel gives

```text
Q_l
 =2sum_x U(x)-Corr(U,B;l)-Corr(B,U;l)+Corr(U,U;l)
  +e^2 sum_i delta_(d_i)Corr(chi_i,chi_i;l).       (L-0315.2)
```

For `d>=2`, `0<=u_d<=4/d`; for `d>=3`,
`0<=delta_d<=C/d^2`.  Each owner indicator is a union of at most two
circular intervals, and

```text
TV_circ Corr(f,g)
 <=min(||f||_1 TV_circ(g),TV_circ(f)||g||_1).
```

A size-two owner has at most one generic phase, so its negative diagonal
correction vanishes at every target lag.  These facts prove the uniform
statewise theorem

```text
sum_(l=2)^(n-3)|Delta Q_l(s)|
 <=C sum_(i:d_i>=2)1/d_i.                          (L-0315.3)
```

For the already-combined source coefficient

```text
omega(s)=a(s)[C_B(s)/A_B-C_A(s)/A_A],
```

the exact positive-composition expansion uses coefficient `1/3` for the
triangle row and `1/4` for each separated/nested double orientation.
The cyclic root already supplies the anchor, so there is no extra factor
`n`.  All T/S/N owner rows and IS/IN/X incidences remain inside the signed
coefficient before any norm.

Because `sum_s|omega(s)|<=2`, applying (L-0315.3) only after the complete
signed combination proves that all states whose residual owners are each at
least `(k+1)/2` contribute `O(1/k)`.  Consequently the complete
decorated owner-ratio derivative is equivalent to the standalone boundary
tail:

> **BODT.** Restrict the same signed positive-composition `Delta Q` vector
> to states having a residual owner at most `(k-1)/2`; prove its lag l1
> norm is `O(1/k)`.

The equivalence is exact because the removed macroscopic-owner vector is
already `O(1/k)`.  BODT is decorated-only; the collision vector is the
separately accepted CIBV.

The sole predeclared exact `k=9` check reconstructs source masses one,

`k DOR=2.505617585...` and exact `DOR=DTR` at this rank only.  This is
finite calibration, not an all-rank DSR/DTR theorem.

### L-0317 - Common schedules are simultaneous chart-stabilizer transporters

M296 fixes the literal doubled-star chart

```text
c=c_d=(1,2)^2(1,3)^2...(1,d)^2,
S=BStab_d={s in B_(2d-2):c^s=c},
L_d=50d(2d-2)^3.
```

For every actual matching-selected edge `Q`, the accepted individual
source and target frames give

```text
F_Q={beta:u_Q^beta=v_Q}=alpha_Q;S;delta_Q^(-1),
|alpha_Q|,|delta_Q|<=L_d.
```

Fixing one reference edge `Q_0`, put

```text
L_Q=alpha_Q^(-1);alpha_0,
R_Q=delta_0^(-1);delta_Q,
x_Q=c^(L_Q),
y_Q=c^(R_Q^(-1)),
C_Q={s in S:L_Q;s;R_Q in S}.                       (L-0317.1)
```

The exact residualization theorem is

```text
C_Q=Trans_S(x_Q,y_Q),

intersection_Q F_Q
 =alpha_0;(intersection_Q C_Q);delta_0^(-1).        (L-0317.2)
```

Thus one common schedule is exactly one element of the literal chart
stabilizer simultaneously mapping the full relative source tuple to the
relative target tuple.  This is a two-sided framed reduction, not the
common-prefix chart cover refuted by M291/M294.

For one additional edge, compatibility is exactly

```text
C_Q != empty
 iff S;L_Q;S=S;R_Q^(-1);S.                         (L-0317.3)
```

More intrinsically, two arbitrary fibers `F_P,F_Q` meet iff
`S;alpha_Q^(-1);alpha_P;S=S;delta_Q^(-1);delta_P;S`.  If
`s_Q in C_Q`, then

```text
C_Q=s_Q;[S intersect R_Q;S;R_Q^(-1)]
   =[S intersect L_Q^(-1);S;L_Q];s_Q.              (L-0317.4)
```

Pairwise double-coset compatibility is only a necessary diagnostic for a
whole family; it is not promoted to a higher-arity Helly property.
Changing frames applies one common two-sided translate to every constraint.
Projection to the finite action preserves common nonemptiness but does not
preserve shortest literal braid length.  The exact length comparison is

```text
|beta|<=2L_d+|s|,       |s|<=2L_d+|beta|.           (L-0317.5)
```

M296 also proves concrete literal loops
`R_(2r-1), R_(2r)^3, q_r^2 in S`, where the four-letter `q_r` swaps
adjacent doubled-star square blocks but `q_r` itself is not in `S`.
Choosing one short chart route `t_x` for every `x in Z_d`, the complete
Reidemeister--Schreier rows

```text
t_x;R_j^(+/-1);t_(x^R_j^(+/-1))^(-1)
```

generate `S`; every displayed row has length at most
`100d(2d-2)^3+1`.  There can be
`2(2d-3)d^(d-3)(2d-2)!` rows.  Hence this is a complete inventory of
individually short loops with factorial indexing, not a polynomial-size
generating set, transporter diameter, or schedule cover.

### L-0318 - The decorated endpoint tail localizes to three small-owner rows

M297 starts from the accepted M290/M293 decorated defect field.  For every
boundary state split its owner field as `U=U_S+U_L`, where `U_S` contains
owner sizes `d<=(k-1)/2` and `U_L` the larger owners.  The accepted
inverse-owner bounds give

```text
TV_circ(U_L)=O(1/k),
||U_S||_1,||U_L||_1=O(1).                          (L-0318.1)
```

Using correlation variation, both `U_S/U_L` orientations, `U_L/U_L`,
both `U_L/B` orientations, and every large-owner diagonal defect have
lag variation `O(1/k)`.  This estimate is applied only after the complete
signed product coefficient `omega=P_B-P_A` is formed; since
`sum_s|omega(s)|<=2`, every large field can be removed even from states
that still contain a small owner.  Thus BODT is equivalent, at the
`O(1/k)` assertion level, to the small-owner-local vector SODT.

After collapsing the support anchor with the exact triangle/double
multiplicities `1/3` and `1/4`, define the already-combined scalar lag
profiles `H_d` (small-owner/head correlations), `C^off_(d,e)`
(distinct small-owner correlations), and `A_d` (self correlations).
The exact residual vector is

```text
W_ell=
 -sum_d u_d Delta H_d(ell)
 +sum_(d,e)u_d u_e Delta C^off_(d,e)(ell)
 +sum_d(u_d^2+e^2 delta_d)Delta A_d(ell).          (L-0318.2)
```

Every profile in (L-0318.2) retains both source laws and live channels,
all T/S/N types, phases, seams, and incidence multiplicities before any
row norm.  A size-one owner has no generic phase, so all `d=1` rows
vanish and one may set formal `u_1=0`.  At `d=2,3`, only the self-owner
derivative vanishes; first-order and distinct-owner rows remain.

With `b_d=e^(-d)d^(d-1)/d!`, the first sufficient open lemma is

```text
||Delta H_d||_1
 <=C d b_d/k,

||Delta C^off_(d,e)||_1
 <=C d e b_d b_e/k,

||Delta A_d||_1
 <=C d^2 b_d/k,          d>=4.                    (SOCL)
```

Critical-Borel summability together with `u_d=O(1/d)` and
`u_d^2+e^2 delta_d=O(1/d^2)` proves

```text
SOCL => SOCT <=> SODT <=> BODT <=> DOR.            (L-0318.3)
```

SOCL is not proved.  The sole exact `k=9` computation verifies the
phase collapse and finite row sizes only.

### L-0319 - Channel-one GOCI reduces to one all-middle Hahn--Abel inequality

M300 uses the designated exterior/middle product law to prove the safe
atom-summed identity

```text
eta_1(F_ell)=sum_(f in T(M))nu_M(f)sum_e gamma_k(e)F_ell(ef),
gamma_k=nu_L-(nu_(C_-)+nu_(C_+))/2.                (L-0319.1)
```

The two commuting raw color orders are already summed, so there is no
extra factor `1/2`.  The scalar product shorthand is valid only on this
designated exterior/middle support.

For odd `k`, every middle chord is uniquely `f_(a,u)` with
`a in Z/kZ` and `1<=u<k/2`.  With its positive per-chord weight
`w_(k,u)`, define the full phase-orbit coefficient

```text
P_(k,ell)(u)=sum_(a in Z/kZ)sum_e gamma_k(e)F_ell(e f_(a,u)).
```

Then exactly

```text
eta_1(F_ell)=sum_(1<=u<k/2)w_(k,u)P_(k,ell)(u).    (L-0319.2)
```

The sufficient candidate `MCLP_1` is `P_(k,ell)(u)>=0`; it retains all
middle phases and is strictly weaker than refuted fixed-middle positivity.

M300 next classifies the complete exterior Hahn support.  For
`D=2k-1`, with exterior blocks of sizes `k-1,k`, every cross chord has
positive coefficient `v_D(c)`, every seam chord has coefficient
`-v_k(c)/2`, and every internal chord has coefficient
`v_D(c)-v_k(c)/2<0`.  The last sign follows from strict decrease of
`tau(d-1)/tau(d)`.  There are exactly `k(k-1)` positive and
`k(k-1)` negative chords, with equal total masses.

After averaging the response over every middle phase, group the exterior
atoms by cut `c` into normalized cross/internal-seam means
`Xbar_c,Ybar_c`.  With

```text
x_c=2c v_D(c),
y_c=(k-c)v_k(c)-(D-2c)v_D(c)>0,
r_c=x_c-y_c,
R_c=sum_(d<=c)r_d,       R_(k-1)=0,
```

summation by parts gives the exact identity

```text
P_(k,ell)(u)
 =sum_(c=1)^(k-1)y_c(Xbar_c-Ybar_c)
  +sum_(c=1)^(k-2)R_c(Xbar_c-Xbar_(c+1)).         (EHA_1)
```

This is a reduction, not a sign proof.  At the sole predeclared `k=7`
row the cumulative signs are `---++0`.  All 54 cut-class/lag totals are
positive, but 44 of 378 fixed-middle cells are negative; all complete
`eta_1` lags remain positive.  These are exact finite facts only.

### L-0320 - Pairwise fiber compatibility is not a common-action theorem

For an actual M135 perfect matching `M`, M301 defines finite action fibers
`K_Q`, agreement hyperedges

```text
E_a(M)={Q in M:a in K_Q},
```

and the action-cover number `kappa(M)`.  It proves exactly that `kappa(M)`
is the minimum number of subfamilies partitioning `M` with a nonempty total
fiber intersection in every cell.

For nonempty `U subset M`, put

```text
rho(U)=max_a |E_a(M) intersect U|/|U|.
```

The exact greedy equivalence is

```text
kappa(M)<=q
 => rho(U)>=1/q for every nonempty U,

rho(U)>=1/q for every nonempty U
 => kappa(M)<=ceil(q(1+ln|M|)).                    (L-0320.1)
```

Since

```text
|M|=d^(d-3)(2d-2)!/d!,       ln|M|=O(d log d),
```

inverse-polynomial residual agreement density is polynomially equivalent
to a polynomial finite common-action cover.  Under finite-action
transitivity on `Z_d`, double counting gives exact mean agreement
`|M|/|Z_d|=1/d!`; this is not a maximum-agreement bound.

M301 also supplies a strict actual-edge firewall at `d=3`.  In one actual
matching `[0,1,2,9]`, the subfamily `[0,2,9]` has

```text
K_0 intersect K_2={45},
K_0 intersect K_9={49},
K_2 intersect K_9={89},
K_0 intersect K_2 intersect K_9=empty.            (L-0320.2)
```

Thus hereditary 2-Helly and the unqualified pairwise-graph shortcut are
refuted in the actual edge universe.  However, none of the 36 complete
`d=3` matching occurrences is pairwise compatible, so the registered
whole-matching MT2H implication is vacuous at this guard and is not
refuted.

The first matching-specific all-rank lemma is MRAD: for every `d>=6`,
actual perfect matching, and nonempty residual `U`, some finite action
must agree with at least `|U|/d^C` selected edges.  MRAD remains open.
Even MRAD only supplies finite-action nonemptiness; polynomial literal
braid representative length remains a separate SSTID requirement.

### L-0321 - Canonical quantile transport requires multi-cell cancellation

For M300/M303's exact all-middle channel-one cut law, M307 defines the
positive and negative cumulative mass intervals

```text
I_c=(A_(c-1),A_c],       J_d=(B_(d-1),B_d],
pi_(c,d)=|I_c intersect J_d|.
```

The two interval families partition the same total Hahn mass, so

```text
sum_d pi_(c,d)=x_c,       sum_c pi_(c,d)=y_d.
```

Consequently the complete cut-class response has the exact increasing
quantile representation

```text
P_(k,ell)(u)=sum_(c,d)pi_(c,d)(Xbar_c-Ybar_d).       (QTI_1)
```

This is an identity after every middle phase is combined.  With
`R_c=A_c-B_c`, a negative cumulative coefficient forces positive
transport mass through cells `i>c>=j`, while a positive coefficient
forces cells `i<=c<j`.  Thus the accepted mixed Abel signs are preserved
as transport across both diagonal directions; quantile coupling does not
turn them into a one-direction monotonicity statement.

M307 predeclares the sufficient cellwise candidate

```text
QCP_1: Xbar_c>=Ybar_d whenever pi_(c,d)>0.
```

Its sole exact new guard, `k=9,n=27`, refutes QCP_1.  The first
lexicographic witness is `(u,ell,c,d)=(1,2,2,2)`, with
`pi_(2,2)>0` and `Xbar_2-Ybar_2<0` (approximately
`-2.871982675510025`; the exact rational is frozen in M307 equation
`(5.1)`).  Exactly 778 of 1440 supported cells are negative, and every
one of the 96 cut-class/lag rows contains a negative cell.

Nevertheless all 96 complete QTI_1 aggregates in that row are strictly
positive.  Their exact minimum occurs at `u=2`, reflected lags
`ell=8,19`, and is approximately `0.0997997722412562`.  This is
finite-only and proves neither MCLP_1 nor GOCI_1; it shows that any proof
through QTI_1 must retain cancellation among multiple supported cells.

### L-0322 - WRAD is the correct weighted cover dual, not a pair moment

For an actual M135 perfect matching `M`, M306 proves the exact anchored
conditional identity

```text
(1/|K_P|)sum_(Q in U)|K_P intersect K_Q|
  = E[|E_a(M) intersect U| | a in K_P].
```

The corresponding lower bound `RCOM` implies MRAD, but it is not
equivalent to it.

Define the fractional action-cover number `tau*(M)`.  Finite LP duality
gives the exact equivalence

```text
tau*(M)<=q
 iff
 for every lambda>=0 some action a has
 sum_(Q in E_a(M))lambda_Q >= ||lambda||_1/q.       (WRAD)
```

Taking indicator weights shows WRAD implies residual agreement density.
Conversely, sorting arbitrary weights and thresholding a maximizing prefix
loses only `H_|M|`; since `log|M|=O(d log d)`, MRAD, WRAD, and
polynomial fractional-cover cost are polynomially equivalent.

M306 also proves a strict generic obstruction to the predeclared
conditional-moment route.  For the natural action of `Sym(X)` or
`Alt(X)`, use the point-fixing fibers `K_x`.  The identity action
agrees with all `N=|X|` constraints, so the action-cover number is one,
but every full-family anchored conditional score is exactly

```text
1+(N-1)|K_x intersect K_y|/|K_x|=2.
```

Taking `N` of the actual matching scale gives a superpolynomial
separation between MRAD and RCOM.  This is a formal route obstruction,
not an actual-M135 counterexample and not evidence that actual RCOM fails.

The accepted `d=3` guard remains exact: the three-edge subfamily has
three distinct singleton pair intersections, empty triple intersection,
anchored score `11/9`, and maximum agreement two.  It has no
`d>=6` implication.

### L-0323 - Scalar Borel variation does not control marked owner profiles

M305 groups the exact remaining small-owner rows only after the complete
signed source, channel, type, phase, seam, and incidence coefficient has
been formed.  For literal owner-response profiles, deterministic circular
correlation estimates give

```text
||P^H||_1<=32,
||P^A||_1<=4d,
||P^C||_1<=4min(d,e).
```

If the corresponding fully combined profile coefficient measures obey

```text
||mu^H_(k,d)||_TV<=C d b_d/k,
||mu^A_(k,d)||_TV<=C d b_d/k,
||mu^C_(k,d,e)||_TV<=C max(d,e)b_d b_e/k,          (CBPV)
```

then taking a triangle inequality only over the already-combined profile
classes proves exactly the three SOCL row bounds.  Thus `CBPV=>SOCL` is
a proved deterministic implication, not a proof of either premise.

For the complete conditioned-Borel split laws

```text
p_s=[(2k-1)/(2k-2)] b_s b_(2k-1-s)/b_(2k-1),
q_s=[k/(k-1)] b_s b_(k-s)/b_k
```

on their exact domains, put `a_s=p_s-q_s` and extend by zero at the
endpoints.  M305 proves all-rank that

```text
sum_s |a_(s+1)-a_s|=O(1/k).                       (L-0323.1)
```

The proof uses exact adjacent Borel ratios on the low half, a
`b_s H_k(s)` representation with `H_k(s)=O(s/k)` and
`Delta H_k(s)=O(1/k)`, and a separate Stirling estimate through the
bulk, junction, and terminal endpoint.

This scalar variation is not enough.  The same coefficient has
`sum_s|a_s|=Theta(k^-1/2)`.  On an abstract two-point profile space,
assigning the negative and positive split ranges to different unit atoms
aligns the profiles with the sign and attains that larger norm.  This
strictly refutes the generic scalar-BV multiplier shortcut without
refuting the actual profile candidate.

The exact unresolved marked identity is

```text
mu^X
 =sum_(u=1)^(k-2) D_u(F^X_u-F^X_(u+1))
  +sum_(s=1)^((k-1)/2) q_s(F^X_s-G^X_s),           (CMPAD)
```

where both live channels and all T/S/N kinds, phases, seams, and owner
incidences are already combined in `F^X,G^X`.  Proving the three CBPV
scales for this one signed measure is the first unproved step.  Bounding
the two displayed sums or any channel/source/type row separately is not
licensed.

At low sizes use formal `u_1=0`: size-one response rows vanish, while
`H_2,H_3` and low off-diagonal rows remain.  Deleting a zero-profile atom
is response-equivalent but not itself norm-equivalent.  At the scalar
low/high junction the proof also retains
`p_((k+1)/2)-p_((k-1)/2)=O(k^-3/2)`.  These are M310's two nonmaterial
clarifications.

### L-0324 - Abel sign blocks still require inter-block cancellation

For M307/M308's exact quantile transport, M311 orders the sorted cumulative
endpoints and proves that every supported cell occurs exactly once on a
coordinatewise nondecreasing staircase from `(1,1)` to `(k-1,k-1)`.
For a cell `(c,d)`, put `h=min(c,d)`.  The Abel cumulative sign
`R_h=A_h-B_h` fixes every off-diagonal rung direction:

```text
R_h<0 => c>d,
R_h>0 => c<d,
R_h=0 => no off-diagonal rung-h cell.
```

Let `M_h=max(A_h,B_h)`.  For every rung interval `[a,b]`, its supported
cells are exactly the contiguous staircase segment over
`(M_(a-1),M_b]`.  Partition the rungs into maximal constant-sign runs
`H_q=[a_q,b_q]`; their cell sets are disjoint, consecutive, and exhaustive.
Therefore exactly

```text
P_(k,ell)(u)=sum_q C_q(k,u,ell),                    (QBD_1)
C_q=integral_(M_(a_q-1))^(M_(b_q))
          [mathsf X(t)-mathsf Y(t)]dt.
```

The predeclared sufficient strengthening `QBP_1` asserts `C_q>=0` for
every sign block.  QBD_1 proves `QBP_1=>MCLP_1`, but it does not sign the
blocks.

The sole authorized `k=11,n=33` guard exactly refutes QBP_1.  The Abel
sign runs are negative on `h=1,...,4`, positive on `h=5,...,9`, and
zero at `h=10`.  The first lexicographic failure is

```text
(u,ell,q)=(1,2,1),
H_1=[5,9],
C_1<0,       C_1 approximately -0.03599141613051397.
```

The full exact rational is frozen in M311's `obstruction.md`.  The
complete row remains positive, approximately `0.149921663442814`.
Across the sole rank, 154/450 block instances are negative and 118/150
rows contain a negative block, while all 150 complete aggregates remain
strictly positive.  The minimum full aggregate is approximately
`0.09958803705967692` at the reflected pair `(u,ell)=(2,10),(2,23)`.
These finite positive aggregates do not prove MCLP_1.

```text
 =max_(Q_0 in supp(lambda)) max_(s in S)
```

Write the point constraints as `C_Q={s in S:x_Q^s=y_Q}`.  For a
compatible pivot tuple `I), choose `s_I` in its total transporter and
let `H_I` stabilize every pivot source.  M312 proves exactly

```text
T_I=H_I;s_I,
T_(I union J)
 ={h;s_I:h in intersection_(Q in J)D_I(Q)},        (L-0325.2)
```

where `D_I(Q)` is the residual transporter signature.  Witness changes
right-translate every residual signature coherently, and allowed frame
changes conjugate the complete hierarchy, so nonemptiness and common
intersection are intrinsic.

The predeclared forced-cell condition `HSCA` asks a polynomial-size
compatible pivot tuple whose pointwise-forced cell carries inverse-
polynomial weight.  It implies WRAD, but it is not a consequence of generic

and all residual signatures in `G_I(U)` have one common point.  Equation
`(L-0325.2)` then gives one action satisfying the whole heavy cell, so

accepted rank-three non-Helly triple, but remains unproved for actual

### L-0326 - Both marked Abel sums are one antisymmetric endpoint flux

Let `p,q` be M305's exact complete split laws, with cumulative masses
`P_t,Q_s`.  M313 couples them by common uniform mass:

```text
pi_(s,t)=Leb((Q_(s-1),Q_s] intersect (P_(t-1),P_t]).
```

The coupling has marginals `q,p`, support `s<=t`, and exact crossing
mass

```text
sum_(s<=u<t)pi_(s,t)=-D_u.
```

Consequently, for any two profile-measure sequences,

```text
sum_(s,t)pi_(s,t)(F_t-G_s)
 =sum_u D_u(F_u-F_(u+1))+sum_s q_s(F_s-G_s).       (L-0326.1)
```

Thus the two marked Abel terms are exactly one endpoint divergence, not two
quantities to estimate separately.

M313 makes the profile masses literal and equal by adding fixed labeled
slots: three owner slots for H/A and six ordered owner-pair slots for C.
A mismatched or genuinely zero response goes to profile zero.  With
`c_X=r_X(alpha_1+alpha_2)`, normalize the two extended endpoint laws,
couple their common atoms diagonally and their residuals by the canonical
product coupling, and only then sum all quantile endpoints and
antisymmetrize:

```text
J(P,Q)=c_X sum_(s,t)pi_(s,t)
       [kappa_(s,t)(P,Q)-kappa_(s,t)(Q,P)].          (L-0326.2)
```

Both live channels, all T/S/N kinds, phases, seams, raw lifts, and owner
incidences are combined before this edge table or any norm.  The fixed
factor `c_X` is essential.

M313 proves exactly that the divergence of `J` is the zero-slot extension
of M305's marked measure `mu^X`; deleting the zero coordinate recovers
the response-relevant measure.  Since

```text
||div J||_TV<=2 sum_(unordered {P,Q})|J(P,Q)|,
```

the three Borel-scaled net-edge bounds named `MPCF` imply `CMPAD`.
MPCF is the first open lemma.  Scalar path length, tail crossing mass, or
separate residual/channel/type bounds are too large and are not licensed.

The sole fixed guard is `k=13`, row `H_2`.  It verifies the exact
marginals, divergence, and global edge netting and gives finite normalized
values approximately `3.30538` for edge mass and `2.39516` for response
divergence.  This is finite-only and proves no all-rank constant.

### L-0327 - Residual compatibility is an actual tuple-orbit extension problem

Fix the positive-weight anchor and a compatible ordered pivot tuple `I` from
M312.  Write `H_I` for the pivot pointwise stabilizer, choose the node witness
`s_I`, and put

```text
z_Q^I=y_Q^(s_I^(-1)),
D_I(Q)={h in H_I:x_Q^h=z_Q^I}.
```

M317 proves for every further family `V` the exact identity

```text
intersection_(Q in V) D_I(Q)
 ={h in H_I: ((x_P)_(P in I),(x_Q)_(Q in V))^h
              =((x_P)_(P in I),(z_Q^I)_(Q in V))}.  (L-0327.1)
```

Thus nonemptiness is exactly equality of two full tagged tuple orbits, or
equivalently extension of the partial prescription fixing the pivots and
sending each `x_Q` to `z_Q^I`.  Equality-kernel preservation is precisely
well-defined injectivity.  Individual signatures give point orbits, pair
intersections give diagonal pair orbitals, and the common intersection gives
the full tuple orbit.  Witness changes right-translate every signature and
frame changes conjugate the entire system, so every predicate is intrinsic.

M317 names the two remaining actual-family gates:

```text
HPRN: find universal B,C and, for every weighted actual d>=6 instance,
      a positive anchor and compatible |I|<=d^B such that
      V=G_I(supp lambda) has weight >=||lambda||_1/d^C and
      D_I(Q) intersect D_I(R) is nonempty for all Q,R in V;

APOH: every actual residual partial map at such a node that preserves all
      H_I orbitals extends to one element of H_I.

HPRN + APOH => SPBE => PHTC => WRAD.                 (L-0327.2)
```

The implication retains the whole individually extendable set `G_I`, the
positive anchor, and the universal polynomial quantifiers.  A common action
on only a heavy proper subcell proves WRAD directly but does not prove PHTC.

The natural symmetric action extends every admissible partial bijection; the
natural alternating action does so with two unused points, while parity is
the exact zero/one-slack obstruction.  These are calibration theorems, not
claims about the actual chart action.  Exact rank-three facts separately show
that M135 conflict independence need not give pair compatibility and that
pairwise transporter intersections need not have a common point.  Neither
boundary fact refutes actual `d>=6` HPRN or APOH.

### L-0330 - Residual pivots expose exact triple shadows, not induced subgraphs

At a compatible residual node `I`, let

```text
D_I(Q)={h in H_I:x_Q^h=z_Q^I},
V_I={Q:D_I(Q) nonempty}.
```

Choose `P in V_I`, choose `h_P in D_I(P)`, extend the pivot tuple to
`I'=I union {P}`, and use witness `h_P;s_I`.  M324 proves the exact formula

```text
D_(I')(Q)
 =(D_I(P) intersect D_I(Q));h_P^(-1).                (L-0330.1)
```

Hence the child residual set is exactly the parent's compatibility
neighborhood

```text
V_(I')=C_I(P),
```

and for two surviving vertices

```text
D_(I')(Q) intersect D_(I')(R)
 =(D_I(P) intersect D_I(Q) intersect D_I(R));h_P^(-1).
                                                               (L-0330.2)
```

Thus child bad pairs are empty parent triple intersections.  The child graph
is not generally the induced subgraph of the parent pair-incompatibility
graph.  With weighted bad-pair mass `Phi_I`, total weight `W_I`, and
`rho_I=2Phi_I/W_I^2`, the exact child quantities are

```text
W_(I')=c_I(P),
Phi_(I')=T_I(P),
rho_(I')=2T_I(P)/c_I(P)^2.                           (L-0330.3)
```

The predeclared maximum-neighborhood weighted-conflict descent fails twice on
the actual formulas.  The normalized outer anchor and every forced or already
pivoted vertex have `D_I(P)=H_I`, so they are isolated and have
`c_I(P)=W_I`; a global maximizer selects such an inactive vertex and stalls.
Also the actual perfect-matching support has cardinality

```text
|M|=d^(d-3)(2d-2)!/d!,
```

so no fixed polynomial bound on `|supp(lambda)|` holds under uniform weights
and one-vertex deletion cannot certify polynomial pivot depth.

An exact rank-three boundary example has a three-leaf incompatibility star
whose maximum-compatible active leaf deletes the center but regenerates one
child edge from an empty triple intersection.  Its normalized conflict density
increases from `600/90601` to `2/9`.  This refutes pair-graph density as a
formal Lyapunov function, but is not an actual `d>=6` HPRN counterexample.

M324 isolates the next all-rank actual-family statement, anchored active
triple-shadow descent `AATSD`.  At every nonempty child graph it asks for an
active pivot with inverse-polynomial decrease of `rho` and a telescoping bound

```text
log(W_t/W_(t+1))
 <=C_1 log(d)(rho_t-rho_(t+1)).                      (L-0330.4)
```

together with a universal heavy initial anchor.  These conditions would give
polynomial depth and weight loss and hence HPRN.  AATSD is unproved.

### L-0332 - Regenerated triple shadows cannot be paid by an unweighted local charge

At an actual residual pivot, write `rho` and `rho'` for the normalized parent
and child bad-pair masses from `(L-0330.3)`. M328 predeclares `MIDEC`, a
fractional transport of normalized child-edge demand to normalized parent-edge
capacity along the literal M135 orbit-incidence multigraph, with unused reserve
`rho-rho'`. The exact demand and capacity sums are

```text
sum demand = rho',   sum capacity = rho.             (L-0332.1)
```

Consequently the displayed MIDEC reserve conditions would imply both the
absolute AATSD density decrement and the logarithmic weight-loss charge.

MIDEC is formally impossible for arbitrary weights. If pivoting at `P`
genuinely regenerates a child edge `{Q,R}`, its demand contains the fixed
factor `lambda_Q lambda_R`. Every parent edge deleted by the pivot has at
least one endpoint outside the child compatibility neighborhood. Keep
`lambda_Q,lambda_R` fixed and multiply every outside weight by `epsilon`.
Then the regenerated demand stays positive while every deleted capacity tends
to zero. Retained capacities asymptotically pay only retained child demands,
leaving a strict regenerated deficit. This obstruction persists even if the
alternating-cycle support restriction is removed: unweighted incidence paths
cannot repair the coordinate mismatch.

This is a quantifier obstruction to a source-local, weight-independent charge.
It neither produces an actual regenerative pivot at `d>=6` nor rules out a
weight-dependent pivot that avoids all heavy regenerated pairs. The exact
rank-three realization is boundary evidence only.

The strictly weaker next interface `RATSD` asks, at each nonterminal child,
for an active pivot satisfying

```text
log(W/W') <= C log(d)(rho-rho'),
rho-rho' >= d^(-B) rho.                              (L-0332.2)
```

Even `(L-0332.2)` needs a separate actual low-density terminality theorem and
final-step weight control; by itself it gives no heavy residual cell.

### L-0334 - Raw source involution does not imply reverse residual landing

For every supported residual cell `(s,t)`, define

```text
a_st(P)=[g_s(P)-f_t(P)]_+,
b_st(P)=[f_t(P)-g_s(P)]_+.                           (L-0334.1)
```

The two supports are disjoint. Hence every positive directed residual token
`P->Q` in this cell satisfies

```text
a_st(P)b_st(Q)>0  implies  a_st(Q)b_st(P)=0.          (L-0334.2)
```

Equation `(L-0334.2)` is the exact same-cell landing obstruction: merely
swapping endpoint profiles cannot produce a reverse token in the same
canonical residual-product law.

M335 enlarges the literal source-geometric exception to

```text

 Touch_A or Touch_B or BadPref_A or BadPref_B.        (L-0334.3)
```

Off `(L-0334.3)`, both opposite-source raw prefixes exist, and the product-
fixed source switch is a literal raw-label involution that preserves the
unordered response-profile pair and reverses its formal order. That still
does not make it an involution of the residual token measure.

The sole predeclared `k=9,H_2` guard finds an exact token in cell `(1,1)`.
Its A endpoint is the zero profile from channel two, split one, chords
`(3,4),(4,5)`, marked slot one; its B endpoint has profile

slot two. Original and switched endpoint owner/head sets avoid

mass. Thus the token and its raw switched pair are outside `Exc_pf^*`.

The original token has exact positive weight

```text
/160127614349801894448349798657905382956928144305646616084388152620616845.

```

Both switched splits remain one, so the proposed reverse also lies in cell
`(1,1)`. But `(L-0334.2)` gives exactly

```text

```

The reverse residual token is absent. The frozen `S_pf^*` tokenwise
coboundary therefore fails at landing, before its weight ratio is defined.

The exact indicator partition

```text

```

survives, as do the raw-label involution facts. Exact marginalization gives

```text

 <=c_X [ k/(k-1) sum_(s<=h) b_s R_k(s) E_A^X(s)
        +(2k-1)/(2k-2) sum_(t<=k-1) b_t R_(2k-1)(t) E_B^X(t)].
```

The required H/A/C estimates for `(L-0334.7)` remain unproved. Even those

### L-0336 - Exact weighted pivot averages isolate two higher-orbital lemmas

At an actual residual node, let `W` be total weight, `c(P)` the compatibility-
neighborhood weight of pivot `P`, `T(P)` the whole child bad-pair mass, and
`rho=2Phi/W^2`. M337 proves the exact arbitrary-weight first moments

```text
sum_P lambda_P(W-c(P)) = rho W^2,
sum_P lambda_P c(P)    = W^2(1-rho).                (L-0336.1)
```

When `rho>0`, `(L-0336.1)` implies that some active pivot satisfies

```text
c(P)>=(1-sqrt(rho))W.                               (L-0336.2)
```

This is a weight-dependent heavy-pivot lemma. It assumes neither polynomial
support nor unweighted expansion.

Let `A` be the active pivots and `Sh(Q,R)` the literal pivot triple shadow of
the child pair `{Q,R}`. The whole-shadow Fubini identity is

```text
Xi_A
 =sum_(P in A) lambda_P T(P)
 =sum_(Q<R) lambda_Q lambda_R lambda(Sh(Q,R)),       (L-0336.3)
```

and the exact second moment is

```text
sum_(P in A) lambda_P c(P)^2 rho'_P = 2Xi_A.        (L-0336.4)
```

Regenerated pairs are retained in `(L-0336.3)--(L-0336.4)`; `Xi_A` is not
controlled by parent pair density alone. The normalized min/max consequence
of `(L-0336.4)` is stated only for `rho>0`, equivalently nonempty `A` and
positive active normalizer. At `rho=0` the quotient is `0/0`, while the
undivided identities are `0=0` and the node is separately terminal.

M337 also proves the exact conditional stopping theorem `W-RATSD+HLDT`.
Its hypotheses include a universally inverse-polynomial heavy anchored start,
relative whole-shadow density descent and logarithmic weight payment above a
threshold, a heavy final-step alternative, and heavy whole-child terminality
below the threshold. Conditioned on these clauses, multiplicative density
descent lasts at most

```text
ceil(B_low d^(B_rel) log d)
```

nonterminal steps. The log losses telescope, the final loss is charged
separately, and the final whole child is edgeless with weight at least

```text
||lambda||_1/d^(C_init+C_log+C_fin).                (L-0336.5)
```

The hypotheses of this theorem are not proved. The two actual higher-orbital
lemmas are:

1. `AHTSS`: above threshold, one active pivot jointly gives whole-child
   relative density descent and logarithmic weight payment, or a heavy
   terminal alternative.
2. `AHLDT`: below threshold, a heavy pivot supplied by `(L-0336.2)` has
   `T(P)=0`.

The universal heavy anchor is also a conditional starting clause. Pair
averaging alone cannot force either joint selection statement.
